(the korbat links, built from the parsed ids, an account's from the handle as the notice named it, 7.2); "A moderator decides each item that names something on korbat; a link that names nothing is answered automatically. You get one email with every decision, at most once a day." With `pageUrl` (signed out with an address; the auth module adds it, and adds `termsUrl`, `{APP_ORIGIN}/terms`, to every `notice_received`): "To see where your report stands, ask for a re-examination of a decision, confirm that you sent it, or stop korbat's emails about reports to this address, open this page: {pageUrl}". The contact's mail token sits in the fragment, `/notice/<ref>#t=<mail token>`, so it never reaches a server log, and a receipt that the dispatch retries carries the same link. Signed in: "You can follow it in korbat under Settings, Cases, Your reports." Then "How korbat handles reports, and what it keeps: {termsUrl}."
- `notice_update` (`NoticeUpdateData`, section 5): subject "Decisions on your reports to korbat", or "korbat received your reports" when it carries only receipts. First, for each carried receipt, the first paragraph of `notice_received` and its page-link paragraph, and after the receipts, once, `notice_received`'s terms sentence with `termsUrl`; then one paragraph per line, "{ref}, item {n} ({link}): {outcome}." (a re-examination's line "{ref}, item {n} ({link}), re-examined: {outcome}."; {link} is the address the status page gives for the item, an account's under the handle that was named, 7.2); then, with `pages` (signed out: one `{ref, pageUrl}` for each notice the update has a line for and whose receipt it does not carry, the same link as that notice's receipt), one paragraph per page: "To see report {ref} or ask for a re-examination of a decision on it, open this page: {pageUrl}"; then "No automated means were used to decide." and, when a line is a link that named nothing, ", except for links found to name nothing on korbat, which were checked automatically" before its full stop; then, in this order (each condition is read from `NoticeUpdateLine`'s fields: `reexaminable`; `reexamination` without `unseen`, or `changed`; `automatic`; and signed in or out from `signedIn`):
  - when an item's line has a moderator's decision with outcome `no_violation` or `not_evident` and the item is not re-examined and is either no re-examination or one whose line reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` (7.2; `reexaminable`, section 5), "You can ask korbat once, within six months and free of charge, to re-examine a moderator's decision to take no action: it is decided again as a new report.", followed in a signed-out update by "Use the page linked in this email for that report." and in a signed-in one by "Use korbat's Settings, Cases, Your reports.";
  - by the rule of 7.8 step 4, when a line answers a re-examination and does not read `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, or gives the new outcome of an item a review or a correction changed, "A decision changed on review or by a correction, or given on re-examination, is final within korbat.";
  - when a line is a link that named nothing, "If a link was wrong, send a new report with the right link.";
  - in every update that carries an outcome line, last, "You can also go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live (Article 53 of the Digital Services Act)." (without "also" when the update has no re-examination sentence).
- `moderation_decision` {subject, statement}: the subject by kind and outcome: "korbat sealed images of yours while it decides" for an interim decision that holds `seal_media`; "korbat sealed images of yours" for a final decision that holds one; for every other decision: "A post of yours is under review on korbat" for an interim decision on a post; "korbat reset part of your profile while it decides" for an interim decision on an account; "korbat restricted a post of yours" or "korbat restricted part of your profile" for a final decision with outcome `restricted`; "korbat lifted a restriction it placed while deciding" for a final decision that restricts nothing but ends or lifts a measure taken while deciding, and for a correction of an interim decision; "korbat reversed a decision about your account" for a review or a correction of a final decision with outcome `partly_reversed` or `reversed`; "korbat answered your appeal" for a review with outcome `kept`; the statement's paragraphs from `statementParagraphs` (one data value, one paragraph per line); "Read it in korbat under Settings, Cases. korbat's emails never contain a link to act on a decision."
- `moderation_urgent` (staff) {count, oldest}: subject "korbat: a case is waiting in the urgent lane"; "{count} cases in the urgent lane (child sexual abuse or exploitation, direct threats, violence, public security or self-harm) are waiting; the oldest since {oldest}." "Open the staff console. Until the phone alerts of sub-project 3e, this email is the only alert."
- `moderation_waiting` (staff) {lane0Count?, lane0Oldest?, lane1Count?, lane1Oldest?, lane2Count?, lane2Oldest?, interimCount?}. These are flat strings: a lane's pair is present only for a lane with a case past its time, at least one pair is present, and `interimCount` only when it is not 0. Optional keys are read with `data[key]`, not `need`. Subject "korbat: cases are waiting longer than their response time". One line per lane with a case past its time, in lane order: "{lane}: {count} cases waiting longer than {time}; the oldest since {oldest}.", with the lane printed as "The urgent lane", "Lane 1" or "Lane 2" and the time as "24 hours" or "7 days", and, when any, "{count} cases have held a measure taken while deciding for more than 24 hours; their authors cannot appeal until the case is decided." Then "Open the staff console. Until the phone alerts of sub-project 3e, this email is the only reminder."
- `moderation_burst` (staff) {handle, count}: subject "korbat: many staff decisions in a short time"; "The staff account @{handle} confirmed {count} decisions in the last 15 minutes." "Every staff account gets this email. If @{handle} is your account and you did not confirm them, someone else holds your key: tell the operator at once." "If that was not expected, ask them; if their key may be in someone else's hands, the operator revokes it with staff.js revoke."
- `moderation_reads` (staff) {handle, limit}: subject "korbat: a staff account reached its daily case limit"; "The staff account @{handle} opened {limit} different cases today and cannot open others until midnight UTC." "Every staff account gets this email. If @{handle} is your account and you did not open them, someone else holds your key: tell the operator at once." "If that was not expected, ask them. The operator raises the limit with staff.js limits."
- `moderation_reveal` (staff) {handle, time, count, limit}: subject "korbat: a staff account revealed who sent a report"; "The staff account @{handle} revealed who sent a report or notice at {time}. That is {count} of the {limit} reveals one staff account may make today (UTC)." "Every staff account gets this email. If @{handle} is your account and you did not reveal it, someone else holds your key: tell the operator at once." "The staff console's home screen lists every reveal of the last 30 days with its case. If this one was not expected, ask them; if their key may be in someone else's hands, the operator revokes it with staff.js revoke."
- `moderation_csam_lifted` (staff) {handle, time, what}: subject "korbat: reported images are no longer protected"; "The staff account @{handle} confirmed a decision at {time} that {what}.", with `what` printed as "lifted a seal" (`unsealed`) or "answered a report or finding of child sexual abuse material without a seal" (`dismissed`); "Unless another seal or another open report of this kind still covers them, the images its case reaches are served again as before and staff may open them. After an answer without a seal, whoever is on duty opens them the same day (6.1) and, where one breaks a rule or the law, opens a finding on its post. Nobody at korbat could open those images while they were protected." "Every staff account gets this email. If @{handle} is your account and you did not confirm it, someone else holds your key: tell the operator at once." "The staff console's home screen lists every such decision of the last 30 days with its case. A seal for good is lifted only on the answer of the authority korbat reported the images to, and a seal taken while deciding only when it was taken in error: the operator compares this decision with their R2 records. If it was not expected, ask them; if their key may be in someone else's hands, the operator revokes it with staff.js revoke, and whoever is on duty opens a finding citing R2 on the post or the account and seals its images while deciding."

## 8. The console (`apps/admin`)

3a's page, policy and rules stay: React function components, text-only rendering, one CSS file, every answer parsed with the contracts' schemas (jitless), the key asked only after the console checked what it signs (3a section 9, `apps/admin/test/source.test.ts:81-102`). 3c adds `@korbat/rules` as a dependency (the statement preview). Every refusal and every outcome is worded from `messages.ts` and the contracts' phrases, one table each (P9).

**Routes and links.** The router gains `queue`, `appeals` and `case` (`/cases/<id>`, the id a lower-case uuid matched by `^/cases/([0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12})$`, anything else `unknown`). `ConsolePath` becomes `"/" | "/audit" | "/signin" | "/queue" | "/appeals"` (`apps/admin/src/router.ts:7`), and `nav.tsx` gains `CaseLink`, whose `href` is `caseHref(id)` over a `CaseId`, a branded string only `parseCaseId` (the same pattern) makes. The source test's rule stays "`href={` only in `src/nav.tsx`", now pinning both `href={to}` and `href={caseHref(id)}` and the new `ConsolePath` text (`source.test.ts:64-70`). Images: a component `StaffImage` in `src/media.tsx`, the only file the source test allows `src={` in, with `src={staffImageSrc(caseId, mediaId, size)}` built from two `CaseId`-checked uuids and the literal `"small"` or `"large"` (`source.test.ts:72-74` gains that one exception). The layout's navigation gains "Queue" and "Appeals" (`apps/admin/src/Layout.tsx:60-67`).

**Step-up, generalised.** `step-up.ts` keeps the only `navigator.credentials.get` of a confirmation (`source.test.ts` still pins three call sites): `confirmWithKey(deps, action, send)` reads a marked `/me`, asks the options for `action`, checks them with `checkStepUpOptions` (unchanged), asks the key, and calls `send(stepUp)`. `revokeSessionWithKey` becomes a caller of it, and so do the decision, the review and the reveal. The console builds each action's params itself from what it shows, with the contracts' schemas, so the binding it checks is its own.

**Drafts.** The store gains a draft slot per case (`{ caseId, outcome, ground, ruleId, category, actions, texts: { kind: { id, text, salt, digest } } }`), kept across a sign-in by the same handle and dropped when another handle signs in (3a section 9's promise; `apps/admin/src/store.ts:24-29` has no slot today). Saved texts also live on the server (7.4), so a reload fetches them back and the console recomputes each digest.

**Text from others.** Every string a reporter, notifier, appellant or author wrote is drawn through `UserText` inside a frame labelled with who wrote it ("Written by the author", "Written by Reporter A", "Written by a notifier", "Written by the appellant"), never in the console's own label positions (C2). Each frame has "Show hidden characters", which draws every character outside printable ASCII as its code point, and lists the hosts of any web addresses in the text ("Mentions addresses at: example.com"), which stay plain text (T39). Texts citing R3 or R5 open collapsed behind "Show text (R5: direct threats)". Staff's own texts are framed "Written by staff".

**Screens.**

- **Home** (extended): the lanes with their open cases and oldest waiting time ("Urgent: 2 cases, oldest 14 minutes"); appeals waiting, and how many this person may review ("3 appeals wait; you may review 1; 1 has no possible reviewer now. Appeals need a reviewer who made none of the case's decisions and opened no finding in it."); the identity reveals of the last 30 days (time, staff handle, case link; the newest 100, with the total when there are more), so every staff member sees every reveal (C20); the decisions of the last 30 days that lifted the protection of reported images (time, staff handle, case link and "Lifted a seal" or "Answered a report of child sexual abuse material without a seal"; the newest 100, with the total when there are more), so every staff member sees each one; the choices not to seal of the last 30 days (time, staff handle, case link, "Chose not to seal"; the same bounds); the seals taken while deciding that are in force (time, staff handle, case link, "{images} images on {posts} posts"), so the other staff member sees which images stand sealed and by whom; per staff handle, the images viewed today (T41's exposure count) and the findings opened today; mails waiting more than an hour and mails given up in the last 30 days (counts), shown when any; decisions not released after 26 hours and each count of `erasureOverdue` (7.3), each shown when above zero with the runbook's sentence for it; the emergency switch's state; 3a's sessions, keys and sign-ins.
- **Queue** (`/queue`): a table in the order of 7.3 (lane, age, the target's kind, the rules and categories cited, "notice under Art. 16" when any item alleges illegality, "CSAM: no images" when any unanswered item is suspect, "re-examination" when any item is one, "deferred" under the switch), "Load more", and a refresh every 60 seconds without the activity mark.
- **Own finding** (on the queue screen): "Open a case" takes a korbat link, the rule it breaks and the category, sends `POST /api/staff/findings` and opens the case. The console words `not_found` there as "That link names nothing live on korbat: the post or the account does not exist or was deleted."
- **Case** (`/cases/:id`): the header (target kind, lane, opened, version, "closed" when it is); on a lane-0 case, above the evidence and for everyone who opens the case, the banner "Art. 18: decide now, as soon as you have read this case, whether it suggests a crime that threatens someone's life or safety. If it does, or you are unsure, wake the operator at once, at any hour. Record your check in a note ("Art. 18 check: no suspicion" or "Art. 18 check: operator told at <time>"). Nobody gives this case its final decision before that note exists."; on a case with an unanswered suspect item (6.1, the freeze), above the evidence and for everyone who opens it, the banner "Nobody opens the images this report concerns, here or in the app. This case reaches {images} images on {posts} posts; {sealed} are sealed and {served} are still shown to someone. Seal them now, while an admin decides: they are then shown to nobody, their author included." (from `suspicion.reach`, 7.3); when the case reaches no image, its second and third sentences are instead "This case reaches no image, so there is nothing to seal."; on a post case it adds "Do not open this post in the app while a report of this kind about it is unanswered or its images are sealed: its page also shows the post it replies to and its replies, whose images this case neither freezes nor seals."; on an account case it adds "Do not open this account's profile in the app while a report of this kind about it is unanswered or its images are sealed: the profile also shows posts of other accounts that it reposted or quoted, whose images this case neither freezes nor seals, and images posted since the seal. When this page shows images that are not sealed, seal again."; on an item whose post quotes another it adds "This post quotes another, whose images its card shows; korbat opened a case on that post too:" with a `CaseLink` to `quotedCaseId`; on an added item it adds "korbat added this item: a reported post quotes this one, and its card shows this post's images."; when a count of `suspicion.earlier` is above zero it adds "An admin answered {count} earlier reports of this kind about this target without a seal in the last 12 months, the last on {lastOn}." for `earlier.target`, followed, when `earlier.target.imagesOpenedOn` is not null (a post case on whose post staff opened an image in the console), by "Staff last opened an image of this post in the console on {imagesOpenedOn}.", "An admin answered {count} such reports about this author's profile or posts without a seal in the last 12 months, the last on {lastOn}." for `earlier.author`, and then "The newest post with images this case reaches is of {newestPostOn}."; the console holds no link into the app (the source test's rule, above), so on every case the target's and the items' addresses are text, never links; the **evidence**: the snapshot (the author's handle, the post's text in an author frame, the alt texts, the reply and quote ids as text, its time and state; or the account's handle, display name and bio; "withheld" with no content when 7.3 says so), with "Take a fresh snapshot" (the console then reads the case again) when the live target differs from it, and the live state and level beside it; the images as grey placeholders of one fixed size (a class in `app.css`; no `style` prop, no canvas, no blurhash), each with its alt text in an author frame and its width and height in pixels as text, with "Show image" (small, blurred and grey), then "Show clearly", one image at a time, and none at all with "Images of this post are not shown: a report or finding of child sexual abuse material about it or about its author's account is open, or its images are sealed. Decide what turns on an image on the text alone, or wait until an admin has answered that report." when `imagesAllowed` is false on a post case (an account case shows no image, 7.3); the **items**, each with its source summary (its band `answeredUnseen` worded "answered without the images", apart from the share acted on), ground, category, cited law and explanation in frames, a re-examination's statement in a frame and "Re-examination of item {n}", its outcome once answered, "You decided the item this re-examines. Leave it to another staff member when there is one." when `reexaminesYourDecision` is true, and "Reveal who sent this" (only when the source summary names someone: signed in while the reporter link is kept, or signed out with a name; never for a notice on the CSAM path or a finding); the **decisions**, each measure with "in force" or what lifted it, a seal with "{images} images on {posts} posts ({deletedPosts} of them deleted by their author)" and its list of posts as text (creation time, id, number of images; at most `SEAL_LIST_MAX`, then "and {n} more: use the runbook's query for a seal's posts", the read-only query of the runbook's bullet after its R2 paragraph, Task 12(a)) and, when `no_effect` is set, "changed nothing when imposed"; beside a field reset, "written once; the field as it reads now is shown above"; the **history**; the **notes** ("Add a note", saved as a text of kind `note`, visible to staff only), among them each recorded choice not to seal ("Chose not to seal", with its reason); and the **decision composer**.
- **The decision composer.** Outcome first: Restrict; No breach; Illegality not evident (only when an item alleges illegality). On a post case whose `imagesAllowed` is false (7.3), beside No breach and Illegality not evident, the outcomes of a final decision without a measure, the composer says "This post's images cannot be opened now. A report about an image that you answer now is answered unseen: its sender is told so and may ask again." (the database stores the decision as `unseen`, 6.2 step 6). For someone who is not an admin, on a case whose final decision would need an admin (6.2 step 6: an unanswered suspect item or one citing R5), the composer offers only "While deciding" and says "A final decision on this case, whatever its outcome, is an admin's." For a restriction: "While deciding" (an interim decision: `throughSeq` 0, it answers no item and leaves the case open) or "Decide the case" (a final decision); the ground (a korbat rule or the law), the rule (each with its text and, side by side, its "allowed" and "not allowed" examples, T9), or a legal reference text (a decision on the law names no rule); for the law, the checkbox "Terrorist content (Regulation (EU) 2021/784, Article 2(7))", which sets `categoryKeyword`; the measures this target and kind allow (6.2 `decision_actions_check`: for a post, while deciding, Withhold the post and Withhold one image; deciding the case, Remove and Remove one image; for an account, Reset display name and Reset bio, while deciding or deciding the case), offered whatever the target's level. Beside a measure that would change nothing now (the post already not shown or deleted, the field already reset) it says "This changes nothing anyone sees now. It is recorded as a measure of its own, stays in force should the other restriction be reversed, and its author is told (not for a measure taken while deciding on a deleted post)." On a lane-0 case that holds no unanswered suspect item and whose post is not visible it adds "To record that you read a new urgent item, save your Art. 18 check as a note: a note saved after the item arrived ends its urgent-lane mails. A measure taken while deciding here changes nothing and mails its author at once." On a lane-0 account case that holds no unanswered suspect item it adds "To record that you read a new urgent item, save your Art. 18 check as a note: a note saved after the item arrived ends its urgent-lane mails. Reset the display name or bio while deciding only when it carries what was reported: a reset mails its author at once." On a case with an unanswered suspect item the composer offers, while deciding and to anyone eligible, "Seal the images this case reaches" (a `seal_media`; not offered when the case reaches no image), with the ground preset to rule R2 and the facts and the reason filled from `SEAL_DRAFTS` (section 10): the draft of the facts chosen by the case's unanswered suspect items (a notice or a finding; a post or an account; the `added` draft when all of them are items korbat added) and the reason `reason.deciding`, both saved as staff texts like any other, so that the act is two clicks and the key. The moderator may edit a draft; under both fields the composer adds "Never write that the images are child sexual abuse material: nobody at korbat has seen them." Beside it, "Do not seal now" asks for a reason and saves it as a text of kind `seal_declined` (7.4, 7.12), under the words "Record why you do not seal now. This ends the urgent mails for the reports that have arrived so far, until an image is posted that this case reaches; an admin still answers the case. Every staff member sees this choice on the home screen for 30 days." The ordinary measures for the post's text stay offered. While the case reaches a served image the composer says, in place of the two lane-0 additions above, each of them whole, "Only the seal, a recorded choice not to seal, or an admin's answer ends the urgent mails."; when it reaches none, "To record that you read a new urgent item, save your Art. 18 check as a note: a note saved after the item arrived ends its urgent-lane mails." Under "Decide the case", for admins only: the same seal for good, with the reason `reason.final`, or No breach or Illegality not evident, with "A final decision without a seal ends this case's freeze: staff may then open these images unless another open report or a seal still covers them, and every staff account is mailed." and, for either answer, "You decide without seeing the images. Seal for good, and report, when the report's words, the post's text or alt texts, or the account's conduct give a concrete reason to believe the report; answer without a seal when they give none. Say which it was in the facts." and, on a post case, "Your answer also answers the reports on other grounds that arrived before the newest report of this kind, unseen; their senders are told so and may ask again." (on an account case such reports concern the display name and bio, which the admin reads: section 10), and "After an answer without a seal the case's images may be opened: whoever is on duty opens the case's target the same day and, where an image breaks a rule or the law, opens a finding on the post and decides it (if it is what the report said, a finding citing R2, which freezes again). When a new report of this kind arrives before that decision is made, the admin's next answer carries the measure for what staff saw, and its facts say when the images were seen. An admin's answer never restricts under another rule on a report's words alone about an image nobody at korbat has opened: that report is answered unseen, and its sender may ask again." "Lift the seal" (an `unseal_media`) is offered to admins only, in a correction or a review. Then the category (preset from the rule, `RULE_DEFAULT_CATEGORY`); the facts and the reason (saved texts, with the server's refusals shown in place: too long, quotes a report), with, under both fields, "Write only about the author's content and conduct. Never copy another person's name, handle, address or other personal data here: describe it (for example "the home address of another person"). The author receives this text by email and keeps it while their account exists."; and the **preview**, the statement exactly as the author will receive it, from `statementParagraphs` with the drafts. A final decision answers through the newest item the page showed; new items that arrived since stay open, and the page says so after the decision. On a case that holds an unanswered suspect item it answers through the newest unanswered suspect item, not through the newest item (7.5 step 1): the reports on other grounds that arrived after that item stay open, to be judged with the images once the freeze has ended, and the page says so beside the composer, never in the preview, and after the decision. A final decision that answers a suspect item must answer every suspect item of the case: when one arrived since, it is refused (`case_changed`) and the page is read again. A final decision shows in the preview which measures taken while deciding end with it: a seal taken while deciding ends with the admin's answer to the case's suspect items, also when the case stays open, and every other measure taken while deciding with the decision that closes the case (6.1 `measures_in_force`); one that leaves the case open may lift a withhold taken while deciding ("Lift" beside each); a reset taken while deciding is lifted only by "Lift what was done while deciding" (a correction of its decision). "Confirm with your staff key" confirms; a refusal (`case_changed`, `profile_changed`, `staff_recused`, `staff_target`, `admin_required`, `decision_cap`, `snapshot_mismatch`, `snapshot_erased`, `text_not_found`, `invalid_decision`) is worded in place and the draft kept.
- **Lifting and correcting.** Beside each interim decision with a measure in force, "Lift what was done while deciding" opens a correction of it (reversals only, a review-reason text, the preview), offered to anyone eligible, with "Lift the seal" inside it for admins only, and there only for a seal that was taken in error (6.2, the seal). Beside each final restriction with a measure in force, "Correct this decision" does the same, for admins only where 7.7 says so, and with the review composer's sentence on the reason (Appeals, below) where it lifts a seal for good.
- **Appeals** (`/appeals`): pending appeals newest first, each with its case link, the decision, its deadline, and whether this person may review (the statement is read on the case page, 7.7); the **review composer** on the case page: Keep, Reverse in part, Reverse; the reversals of the decision's measures still in force, "Lift the seal" only for an admin, and the whole composer only for an admin when the review needs one (7.7), with "A seal for good is lifted only on the authority's answer that the images are not child sexual abuse material. Say in the reason that the authority has answered, and the date of its answer. Never write its reference or anything that names a person: the reference stays in the operator's records, off the server."; the review reason (under it, the decision composer's sentence on personal data); the preview of the review's statement. The review composer words `snapshot_erased` as the decision composer does.
- **Reveal**: a dialog with the reason category (`impersonation_claim`, `abuse_of_reporting`, `legal_requirement`, `other`) and a reason text, confirmed with the key; the answer shows the handle or the given name once, in a frame "Revealed to you; recorded and shown to every staff member".

**Messages.** `messages.ts` words every new code (section 10); the console never shows a server message (`apps/admin/src/messages.ts:3-7`). `staff_recused` is worded without saying whether the conflict concerns the author or a reporter: "You cannot open or decide this case: an account linked to you takes part in it, or follows, blocks, mutes or replied to someone who does, or the post replies to or quotes a post of an account linked to you. Another staff member must handle it."

**The browser check.** `apps/admin/test/browser/` gains fixture routes for every new endpoint with hostile strings (the longest texts allowed, bidirectional overrides, look-alike console labels such as "korbat: verified notice from police", `javascript:` and look-alike addresses), a fixture WebP of 2 by 2 pixels, and runs: the queue; a case with an image revealed and shown clearly; a case with `imagesAllowed` false (no image request is made); a case with a re-examination item; an account case holding an unanswered suspect item, sealed from the composer with the fixed drafts, and a second such case on which the choice not to seal is recorded (no image request is made, and the page holds no link into the app); a decision whose step-up options bind other params, refused before the key is asked; a confirmed decision; a lift of an interim measure; a review; a reveal; a draft surviving a sign-in. It still fails on any policy or Trusted Types report, page error or dialog (3a section 12).

## 9. The app (`apps/app`)

`@korbat/rules` becomes a dependency (the statement, the rule texts and the measure labels). The report form and the notice status page are outside `settings/`, because `settings/_layout.tsx` sends signed-out visitors to sign-in (`:6-12`). The case screens are inside it (`apps/app/src/app/settings/cases/index.tsx` and `settings/cases/[id].tsx`), so that layout's sign-in and onboarding redirects (`:9-10`) cover them and `AppShell`'s Settings entry (`apps/app/src/components/AppShell.tsx:36`, `match: "/settings"`) marks them. Every outcome, redress and re-examination sentence comes from section 10's constants (P9).

- **Report controls.** `PostCard` gains "Report" in its action row for posts not the viewer's, signed in or out (`apps/app/src/components/social/PostCard.tsx:190-197`), opening `/report?post=<id>`; `MediaViewer` gains "Report this image" (`/report?post=<id>&media=<id>`). Its `<id>` is the post that holds the image: `MediaGrid` and `MediaViewer` gain a `postId` prop, which `PostCard` sets to `post.id` and `QuoteEmbed` (`PostCard.tsx:36-46`) sets to `quote.id`, so an image shown inside a quote is reported on the quoted post. The profile page gains "Report" beside Mute and Block (`apps/app/src/app/u/[handle]/index.tsx:135-144`; signed out too), opening `/report?account=<handle>`. The terms page gains a link "Report something on korbat" to `/report` below its sections (`apps/app/src/app/terms.tsx`), reachable signed in or out.
- **The report form** (`/report`): "What is wrong with it?" with three choices of equal weight: "It breaks a korbat rule" (then the rule, each with its title and one line, R2 replaced by "Child sexual abuse material: choose the third answer, which is anonymous.", and an optional "Anything the moderator should know" of up to 2000 characters), "It is illegal" (then the category, default "I am not sure", with directly below it the line "For the sexual abuse or exploitation of a child, child sexual abuse material or grooming, choose the third answer, which is anonymous."; "Which law or country (optional)"; "Why is it illegal?" (required); and, below, an optional korbat rule it also breaks, R2 not among them), and "It is sexual abuse or exploitation of a child, child sexual abuse material, or grooming of a child" (then which: "Sexual images or videos of a child" (`csam`), "Such images made or altered by software" (`deepfake`), "An adult seeking sexual contact with a child" (`grooming`) or "Other sexual abuse or exploitation of a child, such as offering a child for sex" (`abuse`); an optional explanation; the good-faith checkbox, optional on this path; and "This report is anonymous: korbat does not record who sent it, not even your account. Keep the link we show you to follow it."). When the choice is `csam` or `deepfake` and the only item is a profile, the form adds "If you can, report the post or the image itself: open it and choose Report. From a profile alone korbat cannot tell which image you mean, and its answer covers the images on all the profile's posts." It never refuses the profile. On this path the form takes one profile at most (`CSAM_NOTICE_MAX_ACCOUNTS`, section 10, from which the number in its words is printed); with a second it says, before anything is sent, "On this path a report can name one profile. Send another report for the next one." "Other items" lets the person add up to nine more korbat links. Opened without an item, the form starts with one required field, "Link to the post or profile on korbat" (a `NoticeTarget` of kind `link`). For the illegal ground: the checkbox "I believe in good faith that the information in this report is accurate and complete." (required, Art. 16(2)(d)). Signed out, for the illegal ground: "Your name (optional)" and "Your email (optional)", with "Without an email we cannot send you a receipt or our decision. korbat never shows your name or email to the person you report." Signed out, for every ground: Turnstile, as at sign-in (`apps/app/src/app/(auth)/signin.tsx:4,12`). A privacy line: "korbat uses what you send only to handle this report; how long it keeps it is in the terms. Moderators see who sent a report only when a case needs it, for example when you report an account impersonating you; each time is recorded and shown to every staff member. If the person you report asks korbat for their data, the law may require korbat to give them what you wrote, never your name, email or account." (its second sentence not shown on the CSAM path, which records no sender), and, signed out only, "Cloudflare Turnstile checks that you are not a bot; it sees your IP address and browser details for that check." On success, the screen shows the reference and, for a signed-out or CSAM report, "Keep this link to follow your report or ask for a decision to be re-examined. It is shown only now." with the status page's address (token in the fragment) and a Copy button; signed in, except on the CSAM path, whose intake records no account: "You can follow it under Settings, Cases, Your reports." `human_check_failed` shows "The check that you are not a bot failed. Try again, or email your report to {contact} with the korbat links of what you report and why. Do not attach images or files: korbat does not open them." `rate_limited` shows "korbat cannot take another report from this network or account right now: it takes at most 5 reports an hour and 20 a day without an account, and 10 an hour and 50 a day with one. Try again in about {wait}, or email your report to {contact} with the korbat links of what you report and why. Do not attach images or files: korbat does not open them." ({wait} from the answer's Retry-After, in minutes under an hour and in hours otherwise, as `messageFor` words the daily passkey bound.) `service_unavailable` shows "korbat cannot take reports right now. Try again in a few minutes, or email your report to {contact} with the korbat links of what you report and why. Do not attach images or files: korbat does not open them." On the CSAM path, each of the three is followed by "An email shows korbat your address. korbat deletes your email, and its reply to you, once your report is entered." ({contact} is `EXPO_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL`, a build value that the web image requires, Task 7.)
- **The notice status page** (`/notice/[ref]`): reads the token from the address's fragment (`#t=`), never sends it in a query; shows the received time and each item with its link (an account under the handle the report named, 7.2) and, once decided, its outcome in the words of section 10 (for a report on the anonymous path, the words of `SUSPECT_OUTCOME_PHRASES`, and after an account's outcome `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE`; for a report on another ground that an answer to such a report answered too, the `sealed` phrase where that answer sealed, with `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE` after it for an account; and for a report on another ground about a post that was answered without action while nobody could open the post's images (`unseen`), `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`), a re-examination's answer labelled "Re-examination", and, while the answer's `reexamination.open` is true, "Ask for a re-examination" (a statement box, optional) with "A moderator decides it again as a new report. You can ask once for each item, within six months of being told, until {until}." An item korbat added for a quoted post is listed after the item it was added for, as "The post that item {n} quotes ({link}), added by korbat", with its own outcome and its own re-examination. Beside the re-examination control of an item whose added item can be re-examined too, the page says "korbat examines the post it quotes again as well." Beside an outcome changed on review or by a correction, or a re-examination's answer (but not one that reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, which leaves the re-examination unspent and shows the control while it is open; the rule of 7.8 step 4), it shows "This is final within korbat. You can also go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live."; beside any other decided outcome that cannot be re-examined, "You can go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live." When the page was opened from a mail's link (the answer then carries the contact's `confirmed` and `stopped`, section 10), it shows the contact's state with "Yes, I sent this: keep me informed" and "I did not send this: stop korbat's report emails to this address" (each a POST with the same token), the second followed by "This also stops korbat's emails about reports you send yourself from this address, signed in or not, until you undo it on this page or, if a korbat account uses this address, under Settings, Cases, Your reports." When `stopped` is true, the page instead says "korbat's report emails to this address are stopped." and shows one button, "Undo: email this address about reports again", a POST of choice `confirm`, which lifts the suppression (7.2). Opened from the on-screen link, the page shows no contact buttons. With a wrong reference or token: "We could not find a report with this link."
- **Cases** (`/settings/cases`; `SettingsNav`'s `SettingsPage` (`apps/app/src/components/SettingsNav.tsx:4-11`) gains `"cases"`, labelled "Cases", whose link the row builds as `/settings/cases` (`:23`); the screen shows `SettingsNav current="cases"`): two tabs. "About you": the decisions about the account (day, what was done, for a decision about a post the post's creation time, the rule or "the law", appeal state). "Your reports": the account's reports with each item's link (an account under the handle the report named, also after the account changed it, 7.2) and its outcome once released, its re-examination, and "Ask for a re-examination" while it is open, worded as on the status page, the sentence "This is final within korbat" by the same rule (7.8 step 4). When `reportMailStopped` is true, the tab says above the list "korbat does not email you about your reports, because someone using your email address asked it to stop. Their outcomes are listed here." with the button "Email me about my reports again" (`POST /api/moderation/report-mail/resume`) and the line "korbat may then also email this address about reports sent without an account that give it."
- **A decision** (`/settings/cases/[id]`): the statement from `statementParagraphs` (what was done, to which post, image or field, since when and for how long, and whether it changed anything now; the rule with its text as it stood (the decision's rules version) or the law; the facts; the reason; whether it followed a notice, a report or a moderator's own finding; that no automated means were used; that it applies everywhere; which measures taken while deciding ended with it (for a field reset among them, or one this decision lifts, the field sentence of section 10 and, when `oldValueKept` is true, a link "The decision that reset it" to `/settings/cases/<resetDecisionId>`); and the redress: korbat's appeal with its deadline for a final restriction, the interim text for a decision taken while deciding, the third text of section 10 for any other, with the courts and the Digital Services Coordinator); the post's creation time and the content actioned, as 7.6 gives them; and the appeal: "Appeal" with an optional statement ("Tell us anything you want the reviewer to know. You do not need to cite rules or law.") while `appealUntil` is set, or, when `reversedOn` is set and the author filed no appeal, "Reversed in full on {reversedOn}: nothing of this decision is in force, so there is nothing to appeal."; then its state ("Waiting for a reviewer", the waiting sentence of 7.7 for the appeal's `reviewer` state when it is not `available`, the seal's sentence of 7.7 for an appeal of a decision that sealed images, or "Answered on ...", with the review's statement).
- **Chips.** `StateChip` (`PostCard.tsx:49-57`) reads `post.moderation` when present: "Under review: R4" or "Under review: illegal content" (amber, an interim decision on a rule or on the law), or "Removed: R3" or "Removed: illegal content" (red), linking to `/settings/cases/<decisionId>`. An image of the author's own post shows "Removed" on itself, or "Under review" while it is withheld. Anyone else sees, for a withheld or removed image of a visible post, a placeholder "Image hidden by korbat moderation". A sealed image shows "Sealed: not shown to anyone, you included" to its author, in the post and in the viewer, and to anyone else the placeholder of a withheld or removed image.
- **Errors.** The app's message table (`apps/app/src/lib/api.ts:44-85`) gains words for the app-facing moderation codes; the app shows the server's message otherwise, as today. On `/report`, `rate_limited` and `service_unavailable` take the report form's words above instead of the table's.
- **Terms** (`apps/app/src/lib/terms.ts`): the sections below, without a new `TERMS_VERSION`, their lists of measures, outcomes, limits and periods built from section 10's constants and `@korbat/rules` (a test holds each list to its constant, section 12). The terms page (`apps/app/src/app/terms.tsx`) renders after the sections, before the link to `/report`, a section "Contact" built from `EXPO_PUBLIC_CONTACT_EMAIL`, which is not part of `TERMS_SECTIONS`: "Write to korbat at {contact} when the report form does not work for you, to ask for your data, or about a decision. A person reads every email. Do not attach images or files: korbat does not open them."

**The terms and the rulebook, as 3c writes them.** The draft label and version stay (`terms.ts:5,10`). The texts below are the design's; counsel reviews them in sub-project 4.

- `packages/rules/src/index.ts:1-5`, the header: "The public rulebook. korbat restricts content when it breaks one of these rules, or when it is illegal under EU law or the law of an EU country and korbat is told about it in a notice. Opinions are never a reason. Every decision cites one of these rules or the law."
- R2's text (`:34`): "Any sexualised depiction of a minor is not allowed. Nobody at korbat opens images that are reported or found as such material while the report is unanswered or the images are sealed. After an admin has answered without a seal, staff may open them like any other image. Whoever is on duty may seal them while an admin decides: sealed images are shown to nobody, their author included. An admin either seals them for good, in which case they are kept only for the authorities and reported to them, or answers without a seal. Every final decision on a report or finding under this rule, whether it acts or not, and every review of one, is taken by an admin. Only an admin can lift a seal, and a seal for good only on the authorities' answer; an appeal of a seal waits until the authorities have assessed the images." (R2's automatic hash match is 3d's; its sentence returns with 3d.) Its `automation` becomes `"none"` until 3d, and `seniorQueue` stays true, read as "decided by an admin".
- R1's text (`:25`): the sentence "Images that score high on the automated check are hidden from others until a person reviews them; the author can always see their own post." is dropped, and its `automation` becomes `"none"` until 3d, which brings the classifier and the sentence back with a new rules version.
- R4's text (`:52`): the last sentence becomes "When a moderator finds a report credible, the post is hidden from others while the moderator decides, because the harm is immediate. The number of reports never decides it."
- R5's text (`:61`): "Cases go to the senior queue." becomes "Any moderator may hide a reported threat from others at once while an admin decides, and may lift that again. Every final decision on a report under this rule, whether it acts or not, is taken by an admin, and only an admin corrects one. An appeal against a restriction under it is reviewed by a moderator who made none of the case's decisions and did not report it themselves, and waits while korbat has none."
- R6 (`:67-75`): the title becomes "No spam, scams, impersonation or coordinated manipulation"; the text gains "Coordinating several accounts, your own or other people's, to like, repost or report together is not allowed."; `notAllowed` gains "A group of accounts liking each other's posts on cue to lift them in Explore" and "Reporting someone together to get them removed"; `allowed` gains "Asking your followers to read or share a post" (G10 6.3).
- Terms, "What you can post" (`terms.ts:18`): "You can post opinions, including unpopular ones. korbat restricts content only when it breaks a published rule (sexual content, child sexual abuse material, targeted hatred or harassment based on protected traits, doxxing, direct threats of violence, spam, scams, impersonation or coordinated manipulation), or when it is illegal under EU law or the law of an EU country and someone tells korbat so in a notice. Every decision names the rule or the law and says why, and a final decision that restricts your content can be appealed as the section "Appeals" describes."
- New section "Reports and notices", which states: that anyone can report a post, an image or a profile, signed in or not, from the item itself or from `/report` (linked from these terms), as breaking a rule or as illegal; that a report about a profile is decided on its display name and bio, and the account's posts and images in cases of their own; that a report about a post that quotes another is decided on that post's own text and images, and the quoted post in a case of its own; that on the anonymous path for child sexual abuse material this is different: a report about a profile covers the images on all its posts, a report about a post that quotes another covers both posts, and a report can name one profile at most (`CSAM_NOTICE_MAX_ACCOUNTS`); what a notice of illegality asks (an explanation and a statement of good faith; a name and an email optional, and never asked on the anonymous path for the sexual abuse or exploitation of a child, child sexual abuse material or grooming); that reports are anonymous towards the person reported, whose data request shows what was written only where the law requires it (Your data); the limits (`NOTICE_LIMITS`); that a person looks at every report about something on korbat, and a link that names nothing is answered automatically, which the answer says; the receipts and the daily email (7.8: a reference on screen at once; a receipt at once to a notifier of illegality with an email or an account, except on the anonymous path, which records no sender, sends no email and is followed through the link on screen; at most one receipt a day to an email unconfirmed for 30 days, the others coming with the next daily email; no report email to an address whose holder said stop; decisions once a day by email, and always in Settings, Cases, Your reports or on the report's page); **re-examination** (7.2: anyone whose report a moderator answered with no action may ask once, within six months of being told, free and with an optional statement; it is decided again as a new report, by a moderator, and korbat asks another moderator than the one who decided to take it when it has one; at most five a day; its answer is final within korbat, except an answer given while korbat's staff could not open a post's images because a report of child sexual abuse material about them was waiting or because they were sealed: that answer says so and can be asked again); the order the queue is read in (severity, then age, never the number of reports, and who sent them only under the emergency switch); the emergency switch (when a flood of reports keeps moderators from real cases, the operator may turn it on until the flood ends; reports sent without an account or from accounts under 48 hours old are then read after the others of the same severity, and in the urgent lane (threats, violence, self-harm, public security, and the sexual abuse or exploitation of children) only reports sent without an account; a report is never read after a less severe one, and every report is still read and answered); and what "illegality not evident" means.
- New section "How moderators decide", which states: that a moderator, never a program, decides every case; which decisions only an admin takes (6.2 step 6: every final decision, acting or not, on a report or finding under R2 or R5 or made on the anonymous path, every lifting of a seal, and their corrections), and that any moderator may hide a reported threat from others, or seal reported images, at once until then; that on the path for child sexual abuse material a moderator and an admin decide without seeing the images, on the report and on what korbat can read, and that nobody at korbat opens images that are reported or found as such material while the report is unanswered or the images are sealed, which covers the images of a post while such a report about it or about its author's profile is unanswered; that after an admin has answered without a seal, staff may open them like any other image; that reports on other grounds about those images, corrections and appeals are meanwhile decided on what korbat can read or wait, that an admin's answer to such a report about a post also answers the earlier reports about that post, and that whoever sent a report about a post that is answered without action while its images cannot be opened is told so and may ask again; that the seal can cover every image on a profile's posts, hides them from their author too from the first seal, and never hides a post's text; that a report about a post that quotes another opens a case on the quoted post too; that a moderator never decides about their own accounts or accounts close to them; the measures (`MEASURES`: while deciding, a post or one of its images hidden from others, a display name or bio reset; when the case is decided, a post removed, one image removed, a display name or bio reset; and, while deciding or when the case is decided, the images a case covers sealed); that a reset is written once and korbat does not put the old value back: the person may set the field again, and what they set is judged on its own if someone reports it; that korbat restricts the least it can (one image rather than the post, one field rather than the account); that every finding of a breach is recorded as a restriction of its own, also when the content was already not shown or deleted, and its statement then says that it changed nothing now; that every decision that restricts your content, also one that changes nothing anyone sees now, every decision that reverses one, every answer to your appeal, and every end or lift of something korbat told you it did while deciding comes with a statement of reasons, in the app at once and by email; that a decision to take no action is not sent to you, since it would tell you that someone reported you, unless it ends or lifts something korbat told you it did while deciding; that something korbat does while deciding about a post you had already deleted is recorded without being sent to you, since it can never take effect; and that content restricted for being illegal is restricted everywhere, since korbat cannot restrict by country.
- New section "Appeals", which states (6.3, 7.7): that the person a final decision restricts may appeal it once, while any of it is in force, within six months of being told, free and with no form to follow, with "Appeal" on the decision's screen under Settings, Cases, at most five a day; that what korbat does while it decides cannot be appealed, ends with the case's final decision (shown, sent, and appealable when it restricts; a seal ends with an admin's answer to the reports it was taken on), and may be lifted earlier by a moderator (a seal only by an admin); who reviews (a moderator who made none of the case's decisions, including hiding the content while it was decided, did not report it, and has no link to anyone in the case), and that the appeal waits, without a time limit and saying so on its screen, while korbat has no such moderator: with one moderator every appeal, with two every case both took part in (one hid the content or reported it and the other decided it, which is how direct threats and images reported as child sexual abuse material are handled whenever the moderator on duty is not the admin); that a review keeps the decision, lifts part of it or lifts all of it, and never adds to it; that korbat does not put back a display name or bio it reset (set it again; the screen of the decision that reset it shows what it was while korbat keeps the case's copies, and for at least 30 days after a reset that is lifted or ends before they are erased, unless korbat had already erased a value reset under R4, as Your data says); that a decision reversed in full can no longer be appealed, since nothing of it remains; the seal's rules (an appeal of a seal waits, without a time limit, for the assessment of the authority korbat reported the images to; only an admin lifts a seal, and a seal for good only on that authority's answer that they are not child sexual abuse material); that a decision changed on review or by a correction is final within korbat, and everyone whose report it answered is told; and that you can also go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live.
- New section "Terrorist content" (TCO Art. 7(1)): korbat removes terrorist content as defined in Article 2(7) of Regulation (EU) 2021/784 when it is told about it in a notice, and, once korbat is open to the public, complies with an authority's removal order within one hour; material for educational, journalistic, artistic or research purposes, or for preventing or countering terrorism, and polemic or controversial views in public debate are not terrorist content (Art. 1(3)); korbat uses no automated tool to find it.
- "Your data" gains, with the periods of `RETENTION` (6.6): what korbat keeps when someone reports a post or a profile, whatever the decision (a copy of its text, and its images held; for a report of child sexual abuse material, the images of the reported post, of the post it quotes or of all a profile's posts, kept, shown to nobody once their post is deleted, also when their author deletes the post or the account, until an admin has answered it; images an admin then keeps sealed, with korbat's copies of their post's text, are kept for the authorities, are left out of the account's export, and are not released by korbat on its own), and that deleting a post or the account leaves these copies until their period ends; that a case's copies go about a day after the case closes unless a restriction of it can still be appealed or, for a post, a report that only an admin can answer still waits or its images are sealed; that content korbat removes or resets under rule R4 (doxxing), a post's text, an image, a display name or a bio, is erased for good, as far as that decision still stands, once it can no longer be appealed (six months after you were told) or your appeal against it is answered (for a post, later only while a report that only an admin can answer still waits or while its images are sealed): from your own view, your export and the decision's screen, and every copy korbat keeps of it but its backups (an image once no other case still holds it), also when you had deleted the post, so a later reversal cannot bring it back or show it to you again; that korbat's backups of its database and images hold everything as it stood when each was taken, what korbat erased since included, and are deleted {backupDays} days after they were taken, except a backup that korbat must keep for the authorities, which it keeps until they have answered; that a notifier's name and email are kept apart from the case, never shown to staff or to the person reported, and deleted six months after the last day korbat still owed the report a decision or an email; how staff see a reporter or a notifier (a summary: roughly how old the account is, how many reports it sent, what share were acted on and how many were answered while their images could not be opened, in broad bands, never exact numbers) and when a moderator sees a reporter's handle or a notifier's name (only when a case needs it, for example a claim of impersonation or abuse of reporting, each time with a recorded reason shown to every staff member; the person reported never sees either); that korbat forgets which account sent a report 30 days after its last outcome was given and settled (at most about seven months after a decision to take no action, while it can still be re-examined); that on a report of child sexual abuse material staff see how many such reports about the same post, profile or author an admin answered without a seal in the last {SUSPECT_EARLIER_MONTHS} months, as a number and a day; that the account's export holds the decisions its case screens list and its own reports; and that a person who asks for their data at the address under Contact receives, within a month, every case about them (no-action cases included), the copies korbat keeps, where each case came from (a report by another user, a notice, a moderator) and, where the law requires it after weighing the writer's rights, the words of a report without who wrote it. The existing sentence of `terms.ts:31`, "At sign-in, Cloudflare Turnstile checks that you are not a bot", becomes "At sign-in, and when you send a report without being signed in, Cloudflare Turnstile checks that you are not a bot".

## 10. Contracts

`packages/contracts/src/moderation.ts`, exported from `src/index.ts`. Each table below is the one source its words and numbers are taken from: the console, the app, the mails, the terms and the README runbook use it, and a test pins each (P9, section 12).

- **Constants.** `TDB_CATEGORIES` (the 16 values of G9 2.5, read 2026-10-02), `TDB_FORM_CATEGORIES` (the 14 shown on the illegal-ground form: all but `STATEMENT_CATEGORY_OTHER_VIOLATION_TC` and `STATEMENT_CATEGORY_NOT_SPECIFIED_NOTICE`), `CSAM_KEYWORDS` (`csam`, `deepfake`, `grooming` to their three keywords, and `abuse` to null), `RULE_DEFAULT_CATEGORY` (R1 `OTHER_VIOLATION_TC`, R2 `PROTECTION_OF_MINORS`, R3 `ILLEGAL_OR_HARMFUL_SPEECH`, R4 `DATA_PROTECTION_AND_PRIVACY_VIOLATIONS`, R5 `VIOLENCE`, R6 `SCAMS_AND_FRAUD`), `laneFor({ ruleId, allegesIllegal, csam, category })` (the lanes of section 2), `NOTICE_LIMITS` (5 and 20 signed out, 10 and 50 signed in, 10 items, 2000, 300), `CSAM_NOTICE_MAX_ACCOUNTS` (1: a notice on the CSAM path names at most one account), `SUSPECT_EARLIER_MONTHS` (12, the horizon of `suspicion.earlier`, 7.3), `SEAL_LIST_MAX` (500, the rows of a seal's list in the case answer), `APPEAL_MONTHS` (6, the author's appeal and the re-examination window), `DAILY_LIMITS` (five appeals a day per account; five re-examinations a day per account or network), `DECISION_KEYWORDS` (`KEYWORD_TERRORIST_CONTENT`), `MEASURES` (each `action_kind` with whether it is interim, final or a reversal, its target and its label for the composer, the statement and the terms), and `RETENTION` (the periods of 6.6: content restricted under R4 until its restriction is final, that is `APPEAL_MONTHS` after its author was told or the answer to their appeal (for a frozen post, until the freeze ends, 6.4); intake records 30 days after their last release, reset values 30 days after a lift that comes before the case's copies are erased, statements 12 months after the account's deletion, the access log and reveal reasons 12 months, notifier contacts six months after the last day owed and 13 months at most when nothing moved it, confirmations 30 days, backups 30 days after each was taken (`backupDays`; no code of 3c enforces it: the README tells the operator to delete older backups, except a backup that korbat must keep for the authorities, which it keeps until they have answered, and sub-project 4's backup job reads it), drafts 24 hours, mails given up after five days). The migration's CHECK lists are written from these constants, and a database test holds each such CHECK, as the migrated database defines it (`pg_get_constraintdef` over `pg_constraint`, which `korbat_api` can read), to exactly its constant's values, so the test still holds after a later migration replaces a list; a later list only adds values (6.1).
- **Digests.** `TEXT_DIGEST_TAG` (`korbat.text.v1`) and `textDigest(saltHex, text)`: the lower-case hex sha256 of the tag's bytes, a line feed, the salt's 32 bytes and the text's UTF-8 bytes (G1 5.1; the API, the console and `text-verify` call it; SQL's CHECK computes the same). `SNAPSHOT_DIGEST_TAG` (`korbat.snapshot.v1`), `snapshotText(snapshot)` (the sorted JSON of `{ v: 1, salt, targetType, targetId, takenAt, authorHandle, postState, postCreatedAt, replyToId, quoteOfId, body, media, displayName, bio, handle }`, with the sorted-JSON writer `stepUpActionText` uses, `sortedJson`, private today at `packages/contracts/src/staff.ts:250-266`) and `snapshotDigest(snapshot)` (sha256 of the tag, a line feed and that text). Every time in it (`takenAt`, `postCreatedAt`) is the UTC string that `Date.prototype.toISOString` writes, to the millisecond, as the snapshot answer carries it. `appealDeadline(informedAt)`: six calendar months later in UTC with the month-end clamp. The sorted-JSON writer and the `sha256` helper with its `WebPlatform` interface (`staff.ts:285-297`) move to a module of their own, `packages/contracts/src/canonical.ts`, which `staff.ts` and `moderation.ts` both import and `index.ts` need not export. `staff.ts` imports `moderation.ts`'s constants for the step-up params; `moderation.ts` imports nothing from `staff.ts`: `index.ts` evaluates `./moderation` before `./staff`, so a cycle between them would make `staff.ts`'s schemas read `moderation.ts`'s constants before they are initialised and fail at load.
- **App requests and answers.** `NoticeTarget` (`{ kind: "post", id, mediaId? } | { kind: "account", handle } | { kind: "link", url }`, the url at most 300 characters); `NoticeRequest`, a union by `ground`: `{ ground: "rule", ruleId, targets, explanation?, turnstileToken? }`, `{ ground: "illegal", targets, category, citedLaw?, explanation, bonaFide: true, ruleId?, name?, email?, turnstileToken? }`, `{ ground: "csam", targets, keyword, explanation?, bonaFide?: boolean, turnstileToken? }`, every text under `text.ts`'s rules without a transform; the rule variant's `ruleId` and the illegal variant's `ruleId?` are each one of R1, R3, R4, R5, R6; the CSAM variant's `keyword` is one of the four keys of `CSAM_KEYWORDS`; `NoticeResponse { ref, token? }`; `NoticeStatusRequest { ref, token }`; `NoticeStatus { ref, receivedAt, items: [{ position, link, scope, suspect, sealed, unseen, quotedBy, outcome, decidedOn, changed, reexamines, reexamination: { open, until } | null }], contact: { given, confirmed: boolean | null, stopped: boolean | null } | null }` (`outcome` and `decidedOn` null until released; `position` 1 to 20; `link` the item's korbat address, an account's built from the handle as the notice or report named it and never from the handle the profile holds now (7.2); `scope` `"account"`, `"quote"`, `"account_suspect"` or null, by which the page and the app add `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE`, `QUOTE_ITEM_SCOPE` or `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE` after the outcome; `suspect` true for a suspect item, whose outcome is worded from `SUSPECT_OUTCOME_PHRASES`; `sealed` true, once released, when the item's answering decision holds `seal_media`; `unseen` true, once released, for an item about a post that is not suspect and whose answering decision's `unseen` is true, read from `decisions.unseen` and the item's own `suspect` (the decision was taken while nobody could open the post's images, 6.2 step 6), by which a `no_violation` or `not_evident` outcome is worded with `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`; `quotedBy` the position of the item it was added for, for an item korbat added for a quoted post, else null; `changed` true when a review or a correction changed the outcome; `reexamines` true for a re-examination's item; `reexamination` null for an item that cannot be re-examined (answered without a decision, answered with any outcome but `no_violation` or `not_evident`, or itself a re-examination, unless it reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, 7.2), and otherwise `open` as 7.2 defines it and `until` the window's end; `confirmed` and `stopped` null unless the request carried the contact's mail token); `NoticeContactRequest { ref, token, choice: "confirm" | "stop" }`; `NoticeReexamineRequest { ref, token, position, statement? }` (`position` 1 to 20); `ReexamineRequest { statement? }`; `AppealRequest { statement? }`; `AuthorCasesPage`; `DecisionView` (the statement's data: kind, outcome, ground, rule id and version, legal reference, facts, reason, source (`notice`, `report` or `own_initiative`), measures with their scope and `noEffect` (`null`, `"already"` or `"deleted"`) and, for a `seal_media`, `sealed: { posts, images, deletedImages }` from `seal_posts`, reversals with the measure each lifts, `endedMeasures` (for a decision with `ended_interim`, the measures taken while deciding that ended with it; a seal taken while deciding is left out when the decision itself holds `seal_media`, since the images stay sealed), and, on every reversal or ended measure that is a field reset, `resetDecisionId` (the decision holding the reset: a reversal's `reviews_decision_id`, or the interim decision of an ended measure) and `oldValueKept` (true when that reset's `no_effect` is null and that decision's snapshot's `erased_at` is null or later than this decision's `decided_at`), decided at, `appealUntil` (the author's deadline, `appeal_deadline(informed_at)`, while an appeal is open by `appeals_check`'s rule, else null), `reversedOn` (only for a `final` decision with outcome `restricted`: the UTC day of the reversal row that lifted the last of its measures; null while any of them is in force, and for every other decision, an interim one included, whose measures end with their case without a reversal), `post` (`{ id, createdAt }` of the post its case is about, read from `posts`, whose id and `created_at` survive deletion, the purge and the redaction; null for an account case; never withheld by `snapshot_withheld`), the content actioned or the reason it is not shown (`r4_redacted`, `erased` with `contentErasedOn`, the UTC day of the snapshot's `erased_at`, and for images `sealed` and `deleted_by_author`; the first that applies, in that order), the appeal's state (`pending` or `answered`; for `pending`, `reviewer`: `"available" | "none_single_staff" | "none_eligible"`, the answer of `appeal_reviewer_state`, and `needsAdmin`, the answer of `review_needs_admin`: true only when the appealed decision cites R2 or sealed images, both of which its author was told) and the review's statement); `ReportsPage` (with `reportMailStopped: boolean`, 7.6; each item with `NoticeStatus`'s item shape, its `link` built the same way). `ItemOutcome` and `OUTCOME_PHRASES`, one phrase per outcome used by the mails, the status page and the app: `restricted_rule` "access restricted: it breaks korbat rule {rule} ({title})", `restricted_legal` "access restricted: korbat found it illegal", `no_violation` "no action: korbat found no breach of its rules and nothing illegal", `not_evident` "no action: korbat found no breach of its rules, and whether it is illegal cannot be seen without a detailed legal examination; a court or an authority can order its removal", `not_available` "no action: the link names nothing korbat can show (checked automatically)" (only for an item answered without a case), `partly_reversed` "korbat reversed part of its decision to restrict it", `reversed` "korbat reversed its decision to restrict it"; and `ALREADY_NOT_SHOWN`, added after a restricting outcome whose decision's measures all had `no_effect`: "It was already not shown on korbat, so nothing changed now; korbat's restriction stays in force should that change." (P2 law condition 1); and `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE`, added after the outcome of an item whose target is an account, only when the item is not suspect and its answering decision holds no `seal_media` (every outcome but `not_available`), in the mails, on the status page and in the app's list as `ALREADY_NOT_SHOWN` is: "This answer concerns the account's display name and bio. korbat decides about its posts and images in cases of their own and does not report those here."; and `QUOTE_ITEM_SCOPE`, added after the outcome of an item whose target is a post whose `quote_of_id` is set (every outcome but `not_available`; never for a suspect item, whose quoted post has an item of its own), in the mails, on the status page and in the app's list as `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE` is, after `ALREADY_NOT_SHOWN` when both apply: "This answer concerns this post's own text and images. korbat decides about the post it quotes, shown inside its card, in a case of its own and does not report that here." On the path for child sexual abuse material nobody opens the images, so no item that a decision whose `suspect` is true answers, whether the item is suspect (6.1) or not, and no item about a post that a decision whose `unseen` is true answers without action, is shown words that say korbat found something about them. `SUSPECT_OUTCOME_PHRASES` holds two phrases in their place: `sealed`, for every item whose answering decision holds `seal_media`, suspect or not, since such a decision rests on images nobody opened (in place of `restricted_rule` and `restricted_legal`): "access restricted: korbat sealed its images without opening them. They are shown to nobody, their author included, and are kept for the authorities."; and `no_action`, for a suspect item answered `no_violation` or `not_evident` (in place of both): "no action: korbat's staff do not open images reported this way. An admin found in the report, and in what korbat can read and still holds of it, no sufficient ground to seal images or to restrict anything else.", which is true of a live image post, a text post, a deleted post and a purged account alike. A suspect item answered `restricted_rule` or `restricted_legal` by a decision that holds no seal (a measure on the post's text under another rule or the law) keeps that phrase, which is true of the text an admin read, followed by `SUSPECT_NO_SEAL`: "No image was sealed; korbat's staff do not open images reported this way." One more constant, `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, words an item on another ground that a decision answered with the images out of sight, and it follows from `decisions.unseen` alone (6.2 step 6: a final decision on a post case taken while the post was frozen, through a suspect item of its own case, through its author's account's case or through a seal in force): an item about a post that is not suspect and that a decision whose `unseen` is true answers `no_violation` or `not_evident` (the item's `unseen` is then true) reads, in place of those two phrases, as the `sealed` phrase replaces the two restricting ones: "no action for now: when korbat decided, its staff could not open this post's images, because a report of another kind about them was waiting or they were sealed. If your report is about an image, ask for a re-examination." So a moderator's no-action decision on text alone about a post that is frozen through its author's account's case, or whose images are sealed, tells its sender the truth and does not spend the re-examination. An item that reads it can be re-examined, also when it is itself a re-examination (7.2). An item about an account is never worded this way: when it is not suspect and is answered without action, it keeps the ordinary phrases with `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE`, since its answer concerns the display name and bio, which staff read. `not_available`, `partly_reversed`, `reversed` and `ALREADY_NOT_SHOWN` (after a seal whose measures all had `no_effect`) are worded as for any item. `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE` is added after the outcome of an item whose target is an account when the item is suspect or its answering decision holds `seal_media`, whether the item is suspect or not, since such an answer does concern the images on the account's posts (scope `account_suspect`; every outcome but `not_available`): "This answer concerns the account's display name and bio and, taken together, the images on its posts. korbat decides about single posts of the account, and about what its posts say, in cases of their own and does not report those here." An item korbat added for a quoted post is headed "The post that item {n} quotes ({link}), added by korbat", {n} its `quotedBy`. The `no_action` phrase and `SUSPECT_NO_SEAL` appear on the status page only, since a suspect item gets no mail (7.8); the `sealed` phrase, after it `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE` for an item about an account, and `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` also stand in a mail's line (the reporter's mail for a notice of illegality uses the same words) and in the app's list: the first two for an item of another ground that the sealing decision answered, the third for any item that reads it.
- **Changed app shapes.** `PostView` gains `moderation?: { decisionId, ruleId: "R1" to "R6" | null, legal: boolean, interim: boolean }` (`packages/contracts/src/social.ts:115-125`), set only on the viewer's own withheld or removed posts, from the decision `authorFacts` names (section 5); `PostState` keeps its three values. `MediaView` gains `moderation?: "withheld" | "removed"` (the author's own image), and a post's `media` becomes an array of `MediaView | HiddenMedia` (`{ id, hidden: true, sealed?: true }`: what others get for a withheld, removed or sealed image, and what its author gets for a sealed one, the only case in which `sealed` is set; it carries no size, no `blurhash`, no alt text and no path) (`packages/contracts/src/media.ts:69-77`). `ExportFormat` gains `moderation` (7.11). `MeResponse` is unchanged.
- **Staff answers.** `StaffOverview` (with `mailsWaiting: { receipts, authorMails, outcomes }`, `mailsFailed` of the same three, `releaseOverdue` (an integer), `erasureOverdue: { caseEvidence, intakeRecords, statements, redactions, images, drafts, accessLog, contacts }`, `appealsWithoutReviewer` (a count), `findingsToday` (a list of `{ handle, count }`) and `protectionLifted: { total, items: [{ at, handle, caseId, kind: "unsealed" | "dismissed" }] }`, `sealsDeclined: { total, items: [{ at, handle, caseId }] }` and `interimSeals: { total, items: [{ at, handle, caseId, posts, images }] }`, 7.3), `QueuePage`, `CaseView` (its live target with `state` and `level`; `suspicion: { open, reach: { posts, images, sealed, served, newestPostOn }, earlier: { target: { count, lastOn, imagesOpenedOn }, author: { count, lastOn } } } | null` (`imagesOpenedOn` a UTC day or null, 7.3); with `CaseSnapshot` (with `withheld`), `CaseItem` (with `mediaId`, `suspect`, `quotedByPosition`, `quotedCaseId`, `reexaminesPosition` and `reexaminesYourDecision`), `SourceSummary`, `CaseDecision` (with `suspect` and `unseen`, so that whoever decides a re-examination reads that the earlier answer was given without the images; each measure with `inForce` and `noEffect`; a `seal_media` with `sealed: { posts, images, deletedPosts, list: [{ postId, createdAt, images }] }`), `CaseAppeal` (with `needsAdmin`, as `AppealsPage`'s rows), `CaseHistoryEntry`, and `notes` and `sealsDeclined`, the case's notes and its `seal_declined` texts, each a list of `CaseStaffText` (`{ id, staffHandle, createdAt, text }`), 7.3), `StaffTextSaveRequest { kind, text, salt, digest }` (`kind` any `staff_text_kind` a route saves, `seal_declined` among them; `digest` 64 lower-case hex), `StaffTextSaved { id, digest }`, `StaffTextsResponse`, `StaffSnapshotTaken { id, digest }` (never content, 7.3), `FindingRequest` (`{ target, ground: "rule", ruleId, category }`, `target` a `NoticeTarget`; no other ground), `FindingResponse { caseId }`, `RevealResponse { handle: string | null, name: string | null }`, `AppealsPage` (each row with `reviewer`, `needsAdmin` (the answer of `review_needs_admin`) and `mayReview`; no row carries a statement). No member is named like `email`, `address`, `userId`, `accountId` or `actorId` (the route-table test's `PERSONAL`, `apps/api/test/staff/route-table.test.ts:183`, now runs over these too): sources are `SourceSummary` with `kind`, `label`, `accountAge` (`"under_1_month" | "1_to_12_months" | "over_12_months"`), `itemsFiled` (`"1" | "2_to_9" | "10_or_more"`), `actedOn` (`"none_decided" | "none" | "under_half" | "half_or_more"`), `answeredUnseen` (`"none" | "1" | "2_to_9" | "10_or_more"`: the items that read `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, which `actedOn` leaves out, 7.3), `named`, `contactGiven`, `confirmed`, `suppressed`, `contactLost` (true when the intake has a `contact_id` and `notifierFacts` answers `found` false); people are handles.
