- **Step-up actions** (`packages/contracts/src/staff.ts`): `STAFF_STEP_UP_ACTIONS` gains `case_decided` (target type `case`), `appeals_reviewed` (target type `decision`) and `identity_revealed` (target type `intake_item`), each with a target id `LowerCaseUuid` and strict params of ids, enums, integers and 64-character lower-case hex only, never a free string and never a schema with a transform (repo-3c trap 1; `MediaId` transforms, so image ids are `LowerCaseUuid`): `CaseDecidedParams { kind: "interim" | "final" | "correction", stateVersion, throughSeq, reviewsDecisionId: uuid | null, snapshotId, snapshotDigest, outcome, ground: enum | null, ruleId: enum | null, rulesVersion, redressVersion, category: enum, categoryKeyword: "KEYWORD_TERRORIST_CONTENT" | null, actions: [{ kind, mediaId: uuid | null }] (at most 8), factsDigest, reasonDigest, legalReferenceDigest (each hex | null) }`; `AppealsReviewedParams { stateVersion, appealId: uuid, snapshotId, snapshotDigest, outcome: "kept" | "partly_reversed" | "reversed", rulesVersion, redressVersion, category: enum, actions: [{ kind, mediaId: uuid | null }] (reversals only, at most 8), reasonDigest: hex }`; `IdentityRevealedParams { reasonCategory, reasonDigest }`. Cross-field rules (the actions sorted by kind then media id, without repeats, at most one post measure (`withhold` or `remove`; a `seal_media` is none) and at most one `seal_media`; an interim decision's `throughSeq` 0; the outcome by kind (`interim` only `restricted`; `final` `restricted`, `no_violation` or `not_evident`; `correction` `partly_reversed` or `reversed`); `categoryKeyword` null unless `ground` is `illegal`; `ruleId` null when `ground` is `illegal`; a restriction's ground and digests; a correction's `ground`, `ruleId`, `categoryKeyword`, `factsDigest` and `legalReferenceDigest` null and its actions reversals only) are zod refinements, which keep no transform, and which zod 4.6.5's `.extend()` keeps when `requireStaffStepUp` adds `stepUp`, since its keys do not overlap (`node_modules/zod/v4/core/util.js:449-465`); the triggers check them again. `StaffStepUpOptionsRequest`'s union lists the three (`staff.ts:173`), and `packages/contracts/test/staff.test.ts:230`'s pinned keys become four. A schema test (C7) walks every params schema of the four actions and refuses any `z.string()` member that is not a uuid, an enum or a hex digest.
- **`ModerationErrorCode`** (with messages in the module and words in the app and the console, one table each): `notice_link_invalid` (422), `notice_link_offsite` (422), `human_check_failed` (403), `already_reported` (409), `staff_cannot_report` (403), `notice_not_found` (404), `appeal_not_open` (422), `appeal_exists` (409), `reexamination_not_open` (422), `reexamination_exists` (409), `case_changed` (409), `case_closed` (409), `staff_recused` (403), `staff_target` (403), `admin_required` (403), `reviewer_conflict` (403), `decision_cap` (429), `profile_changed` (409), `snapshot_mismatch` (409), `snapshot_erased` (409), `text_invalid` (422), `text_quotes_user` (422), `text_not_found` (409), `invalid_decision` (422), `image_refused` (404), `reveal_refused` (422). The routes may also answer the shared `invalid_request`, `onboarding_required`, `not_found`, `rate_limited`, `service_unavailable`, and on staff routes `staff_session_required`, `staff_step_up_required`, `staff_step_up_invalid`, `challenge_expired`, `csrf_failed`. Messages that the app shows are written for the person reporting or reported ("You already reported this; it is being looked at."), the console's for staff. The database-only codes `moderation_column`, `quote_item_missing` and `read_committed_only` are not among them: no route answers one, and no API path can meet one but the re-examination route, which runs its transaction once more on `quote_item_missing` (7.2).

`packages/rules/src/index.ts` gains `RULES_VERSION = 2`, `RULEBOOKS` (`{ 1: the six rules as they stand, 2: the six rules of section 9 }`), `RULES = RULEBOOKS[RULES_VERSION]`, `getRule(id, version)` and `REDRESS_VERSION = 1` with its three texts: korbat's appeal (with its deadline, the courts and the Digital Services Coordinator); the interim text, "This applies while korbat decides, and ends with its decision. If korbat's decision restricts your post or profile, you can appeal it. You can also go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live (Article 53 of the Digital Services Act)." (P5 law condition 2); and, for a decision its author cannot appeal, "korbat's appeal does not apply to this decision. Where an earlier decision about this content still stands in part, its own screen in korbat says whether you can still appeal it. You can go to court, or complain to the Digital Services Coordinator of the EU country where you live (Article 53 of the Digital Services Act)." 3e adds a redress version with an order's redress. `packages/rules/src/statement.ts` holds `statementParagraphs(input)`, a pure function of the decision's data (`DecisionView`), the rulebook version and the redress version, which the API's mail, the app and the console's preview share. It imports `DecisionView` and `MEASURES` from `@korbat/contracts`, which `@korbat/rules` depends on from Task 6. `@korbat/contracts` imports nothing from `@korbat/rules`, so the two packages never form a cycle. It picks the redress text from the decision's own kind and outcome, never from later events, so a statement renders as sent: an `interim` decision, the interim text; a `final` decision with outcome `restricted`, korbat's appeal; every other decision, the third text. A review or a correction renders the reviewed decision's rule or law from that decision's row and rules version, and its own redress under its own `redress_version`. It names the source as "following a notice under Article 16 of the Digital Services Act", "following a report by another user" or "found by a moderator". For every decision about a post, the sentence that says what was done names the post as "your post of {createdAt}", printed as `noticeTime` prints times. Its sentences, each true whatever happened later because none reads a later event (P1):
  - a `restore` or a `restore_media`: "korbat lifted this restriction. If your post or image is still restricted, another decision listed in Settings, Cases applies, or its content was erased under korbat's retention rules.";
  - a `restore_display_name` or a `restore_bio`: "korbat lifted the reset of your display name (bio).", then the field sentence;
  - a `reset_display_name` or `reset_bio` whose `noEffect` is null: "korbat wrote this placeholder once. You may set your display name (bio) again; what you set is judged on its own if someone reports it, and korbat does not put the old value back when this measure ends or is lifted.";
  - a measure with `noEffect` `already`: "Another decision already keeps this post (image) from being shown, so this changes nothing anyone sees now. It stays in force should that decision be reversed."; for a field, "The field already held korbat's placeholder, so this changes nothing now.";
  - a measure with `noEffect` `deleted`: "You had deleted this post, so this changes nothing anyone sees."; for a field, "Your account is deleted, so this changes nothing.";
  - a decision with `endedMeasures`: "What korbat did while deciding has ended: {measures}.", then the field sentence for each field reset among them whose field this decision does not reset itself;
  - the field sentence: "korbat does not put back your display name (bio) as it was before the reset: set it again in Settings, Profile if you wish.", followed, when `oldValueKept` is true, by "The screen of the decision that reset it shows what it was for at least 30 days." (when the reset is held by a decision citing R4: "The screen of the decision that reset it shows what it was for at least 30 days, unless korbat had already erased it under rule R4.");
  - a `seal_media` on a post case: "korbat sealed the images of your post of {createdAt} ({images} in all): nobody can see them, you included."; on an account case: "korbat sealed every image it held on your posts at that time ({images} on {posts} posts): nobody can see them, you included.", followed, when the seal lists posts the author had deleted, by "{deletedImages} of them are on posts you had deleted, which korbat had kept under its terms (Your data)."; the numbers are read from `seal_posts`. For a seal for good these are followed by "They are kept only for the authorities korbat reports this to." and, with the appeal's redress text, by "An appeal of a seal waits until the authorities have assessed the images.";
  - a `seal_media` with `noEffect` `already`: "Another decision already keeps these images sealed, so this changes nothing anyone sees now. It stays in force should that decision be reversed."; with `noEffect` `deleted`, the sentence of a deleted post or account above;
  - an `unseal_media`: "korbat lifted the seal on these images. If an image is still not shown, another decision listed in Settings, Cases applies."

`SEAL_DRAFTS` (`packages/contracts/src/moderation.ts`) holds the console's fixed drafts of the facts and the reason of a seal; the composer fills them in (section 8), and a test holds the composer and the preview to them (P9). None says or implies that korbat found the images to be child sexual abuse material, and each reason says that korbat's staff do not open such images, so that the statement meets Art. 17(3)(b) and (e) with what korbat knows. The facts: `facts.notice_post`, "korbat received a notice that reports images of this post as child sexual abuse material."; `facts.notice_account`, "korbat received a notice that reports this account for child sexual abuse material. It does not say which images, so korbat sealed every image on the account's posts."; `facts.notice_added`, "korbat received a notice about content on korbat that shows this post's images and reports them as child sexual abuse material."; `facts.finding_post`, "A moderator reported images of this post as possible child sexual abuse material."; `facts.finding_account`, "A moderator reported images on this account's posts as possible child sexual abuse material. korbat sealed every image on the account's posts."; `facts.finding_added`, "A moderator reported content on korbat that shows this post's images as possible child sexual abuse material." The two `added` drafts are for a case whose suspect items are all items korbat added for a quoted post: they never say that this post was reported, and they name no other post and no other person (T47). The reasons: `reason.deciding`, "korbat's staff do not open images reported this way, so korbat has not assessed them. It hides them from everyone, you included, until an admin has answered the report."; `reason.final`, "korbat's staff do not open images reported this way, so korbat has not assessed them. An admin found the report credible enough to keep the images sealed and to report them to the police, who can assess them." The facts draft is chosen from the suspect items the decision considers: a `notice` draft when any of them belongs to a `user` intake, else a `finding` draft; the `added` form when every one of them is an added item or a re-examination of one, else `post` or `account` by the case's target.

## 11. Security and privacy

What stays impossible even for an admin, in the API and the console, and what proves it (`threats-3c.md` section 6, as far as 3c's scope reaches; 3e carries the rows about suspensions, strikes and the orders path). Each row names the mechanism of sections 6 and 7 that enforces it and the test that proves it (P9).

- **Decide, review or reveal without a fresh assertion from one's own key bound to that exact action, case version and snapshot digest**: every such route stands behind `requireStaffStepUp` with strict params (section 10); `audit-verify` fails any row of the three actions without a valid assertion, whatever its actor (exists for every listed action, `apps/api/src/cli/staff.ts:332-334`); a route-table test requires every changing staff route to stand behind `requireStaffStepUp`, except the three named exceptions (saving a text, taking a snapshot, opening a finding) and 3a's enrolment, sign-in, step-up options and sign-out, listed by method and path.
- **Change a post's or an image's moderation state without a decision**: the ledger and its derivation (6.2): only `moderation_apply` writes the moderation columns, reachable only from the triggers of a decision inserted in the same transaction and bound to its audit row, and from the one-way erasure; the posts' trigger and the column grants (6.1). Database tests as `korbat_api`: a direct UPDATE of `posts.state` to `withheld`, `removed` or back to `visible`, of `moderation_level` or `erased_at`, and of `post_media.moderation_state`, `hold_until` or `erased_at`, refused; an insert into `seal_posts` refused; an INSERT of a post with another state or level or with `erased_at` set refused; leaving `deleted_by_author` refused for every role, the migrator included; EXECUTE on `moderation_apply` refused; an action inserted for a decision of another transaction refused, and one whose decision's stamped id a later transaction reaches after a logical restore refused by the time pairing; a decision without its audit row, and one whose case, decider, outcome, rule, digests or actions differ from its audit row's params, refused at COMMIT; and the function test of 6.5. `staff.js text-verify` also recomputes every post's level and every image's state from the ledger and reports any that differs (7.13), so a change made around the triggers is detected.
- **Change or delete an intake, item, decision, action, appeal, snapshot, text, hold or access row, or choose what a notifier is shown and when**: grants (UPDATE, DELETE and TRUNCATE revoked; only the delivery columns writable); `release_outcomes` the only writer of `outcome_sent_at`, `shown_outcome` and `revised_sent_at`; database tests for each table as `korbat_api`.
- **Write a derived column of a case or a forced column of a decision**: forced or computed by the database (6.1, 6.2), `decisions.unseen` among them, so that no caller chooses whether an answer given while a post's images could not be opened says so; database tests.
- **Restrict without a ground, a reason, the facts and a snapshot, or without the author seeing it**: the checks on `decisions`, the consistency check at commit, `author_mail` forced; integration tests that the author's screen and mail carry every Art. 17(3) element for each outcome and measure, a measure that changed nothing now included (practice-3c 11.6).
- **Decide where one's staff account or a linked personal account is a party or related, review a case one decided in or opened a finding in, take a final restrictive measure on a staff account (an interim measure is allowed), answer a suspect item or an item citing R5 with a final decision of any outcome, lift a seal, correct a decision that needed an admin, or review a decision whose review needs one, as a moderator**: the decision triggers and the stored `needs_admin` (6.2, 6.3); an integration test for each, among them a moderator's correction that lifts a seal, and a final decision through an earlier suspect item while a later one of the case is unanswered, refused for an admin too (`case_changed`). And open a case, its images, snapshots or texts, or reveal a source in it, when its subject is one's own staff account, a linked personal account or an account related to them: `staff_case_conflict` before every case route and the reveal (7.3); an integration test per route.
- **Open an image of a frozen post, or a sealed image, as staff**: gate V (6.1, 7.3): `MediaForCases.staffImage` is the only function that opens an image file for staff, behind the one image route, and it refuses an image that is sealed or erased or whose post is frozen at the request, on every case, open or closed; the console's source test allows one image site. Integration tests (section 12): `staffImage` on the suspicion's own case, on another open case and on a closed case about the post, under each kind of reach (the post's own item, its author's account's item, an added item, a seal), refused, and served again after an admin's answer without a seal. No gate proves that a staff member does not open the post in the app (below).
- **Lift the protection of reported images without every other staff member learning of it**: `moderation_csam_lifted` after the commit of every decision that lifts a seal (`unseal_media`) and of every final decision that answers a suspect item without a seal, both an admin's by the triggers, and the home screen's 30-day list; and, so that a seal and a refusal to seal are as visible as a lifting, the home screen's lists of the seals taken while deciding that are in force and of the recorded choices not to seal (7.3, 7.5 step 4, 7.12, section 8); integration tests (section 12).
- **Show a sealed image to anyone, its author included**: gate S (6.1, 7.10): `moderation_apply` is the only writer of an image's state, and `fileAccess` behind `serve`, `forPosts`, and `readyImages` with the export's second read, name what they serve; one test per reader of `post_media` with a sealed image, its author signed in, over the one enumeration of the readers that section 12 holds (Every reader of an image: `serve`, each list behind `forPosts`, the export's files with an export in flight, `status` and `staffImage`); none gives the file, and none the `blurhash` (section 12; P1 law condition 1, security condition (a)). Text is not sealed, so no reader of `posts.body` has a rule for it. Up to five minutes of edge and browser cache remain after a seal until 3d's purge (below).
- **Put a reporter's identity, a report's words or a count into anything the author sees**: the author's reads never join `intakes` or `intake_items` (a source scan refuses the names `intakes`, `intakeItems` and `intake_items` in `author.ts`, `author-repo.ts` and `statement.ts`; and an integration test seeds a report and a re-examination whose reporter's handle, explanation and statement are distinctive strings, restricts the post, and finds none of them, nor the number of reports, in `GET /api/moderation/cases`, `GET /api/moderation/decisions/:id`, the post's chip, the export's `decisions` member or the `moderation_decision` mail); the overlap check on staff texts, re-examination statements included (unit tests); the statement names only a source category.
- **See a reporter's or notifier's identity without a recorded, confirmed reason visible to every staff member**: the reveal route (7.3: the party check, the reason text, the access row and the audit row in one transaction, the identity read only after the commit; section 8's home list); the daily reveal limit and the `moderation_reveal` mail after every reveal; no staff answer carries an address (the route-table test's `PERSONAL` over the new schemas).
- **Read email, IP, device or phone data, blocks, mutes, likes or bookmarks in the console**: the module boundary (the moderation module imports no auth-schema name; architecture test) and the answer schemas; the recusal check reads relations inside a definer function and answers one boolean.
- **Search freely, export in bulk, or download evidence in the console**: no such route (route-table test); evidence leaves the server only through the command line, in 3c what `staff.js account-facts` and `staff.js access-request` print, each with an audit row, and, for the files of sealed images, by the operator's hand as the server's administrator, on an authority's written instruction and recorded off the server (runbook, Task 12(a)); productions for authorities are 3e's (C38).
- **Act on Explore alone or reduce reach**: no action kind for it (section 6); 2c's tests unchanged.
- **Write personal free text into the chain**: the params schemas (the C7 schema test) and the digests; `text-verify` proves held texts are the confirmed ones.
- **Give effect to an authority's order, or mark a decision as one, from the console or the API**: no order path exists in 3c (7.14): `FindingRequest` has no other ground than a rule (a contracts test), `intakes_finding_legal` refuses a finding alleging illegality (a database test as `korbat_api`), and `decision_source` has no `order` value.
- **Delete an image row of a frozen post or request its files, detach or re-own an image, erase a snapshot of a frozen post, or serve, list or export a kept image of a deleted post**: gate K (`post_media_keep` and `post_media_guard`, with no DELETE on `posts` or `profiles` for `korbat_api`), gate E (`erase_snapshots`, the only writer of `evidence_snapshots.erased_at`) and the image readers (6.1, 6.4, 7.9, 7.10); the same trigger keeps every image a case's hold retains, and the disposal predicate deletes what nothing retains. Integration tests (section 12): every method of the media repository against a frozen post under each kind of reach, by enumeration, so that a new method fails until it is listed; the races of a suspect intake against the author's deletion and the purge, in both orders; a cascade as the migrator raising; a delete under REPEATABLE READ refused; an update of `post_id` or `owner_id` refused for every role; every step of the retention job and an R4 redaction leaving a frozen post's rows and snapshots; that `existing()` still sees retained rows; that a deleted post's kept images are in no view, no export list and no export file, and that `status` answers their owner as for a row that does not exist; and an image an R4 redaction erased (by `remove_media`, and of a post-level removal) that another case still holds answers 404 to its author and to the staff image route, is absent from `forPosts` and the export's list and files, and a later report about its post places no hold on it and copies no alt text. The pins of 6.5 and of the architecture test hold who names the predicate, who deletes from `post_media`, and every caller of `requestDeletion` and `openImage`.
- **Act beyond the daily decision cap**: the decision trigger; integration tests, among them two concurrent decisions by one person at the cap's last place, of which exactly one commits.
- **Write transparency figures**: no table and no route (3d's).

In the database, for `korbat_api`: no UPDATE, DELETE or TRUNCATE on any moderation table but the delivery columns, no UPDATE of the moderation columns of `posts` and `post_media`, no DELETE on `posts` or `profiles`, SELECT only on `seal_posts`, no write to `audit_head` (exists), no USAGE on `auth` (now pinned by a test), SELECT on `measures_in_force` and `case_history`, and EXECUTE only on `append_confirmed_audit`, `appeal_deadline`, `frozen_for`, `post_frozen`, `snapshot_withheld`, `release_outcomes`, `decision_final_at`, `case_evidence_due_at`, `intake_due_at`, `appeal_reviewer_state`, `review_needs_admin`, `staff_case_conflict`, `erase_case_evidence`, `erase_intake`, `erase_statement_texts`, `redact_r4_body`, `sweep_staff_texts`, `delete_access_log` and `forget_reporter` (each granted in its own migration; a function-privilege test lists them, as `apps/api/test/integration/staff-schema.test.ts:383-399` does for 3a's). `moderation_apply`, `item_current_outcome`, `release_case_holds` and `erase_snapshots` have EXECUTE granted to nobody. `korbat_auth` gains EXECUTE on `auth.staff_link_counts` only, and the same test lists it (owner `korbat_migrate`, definer, `search_path=auth, pg_temp`, EXECUTE to the auth role alone). Each erasing function checks its own retention condition against values `korbat_api` cannot set: forced times, release times only `release_outcomes` writes, and an account's deletion read from `auth.users`, whose mark the API's other role, `korbat_auth`, can set only to `now()` and never clear (`users_deleted_mark`, 6.4); so the API cannot erase early. `redact_r4_body` checks the decision's kind, rule, measures and finality, and whether its post is frozen, itself, so a call with any other decision, before finality or while the post is frozen, changes nothing; it, `erase_snapshots` and the keep trigger refuse to act outside READ COMMITTED (6.1). `forget_reporter` needs only the mark, so code running as the API can forget a live account's reporter links only by marking it deleted for good, which the purge then completes. For the migrator, the superuser and anyone with Docker on the host, nothing is impossible; a change to a decision or its actions is detected by `staff.js text-verify`, which compares them with the audit row naming the decision, whose own changes the kept heads detect (3a section 2), and a change of a level or a state by its recomputation from the ledger; the other moderation tables are protected by grants only (G4 6).

**What the controls cannot prove.** The console's check of the step-up binding trusts the page, and the snapshot digest trusts the snapshot the API showed: an API compromised before a report can forge consistently from the start (T52's residual). The recusal check knows only the personal accounts the operator linked (T48's residual). An API under an attacker's control can learn whether an account is a staff member's linked personal account, or close to one: `korbat_api` may insert a case naming any account, or an intake whose reporter is any account, call `staff_case_conflict` on it, or insert a decision by any staff account and see whether step 5 raises `staff_recused`, and then roll back, leaving no row; on cases already in the table it needs no insert at all. `auth.staff_personal_accounts` keeps the link from every reader but the definer functions, not from code that runs as the API, which also holds `korbat_auth` and with it the hashed network address of every sign-in, staff sign-ins included (`auth.auth_events.ip_hash`). After an API incident the operator treats the links as known. A `staff_recused` refusal of a decision, or an appeal marked as not for this person to review, on a case whose subject is neither the moderator's linked account nor close to it tells the moderator that one of its reporters is close to their own account; with the source summaries, that can narrow down who reported (C20's residual). A reporter's summary is the same on every case they reported at that moment; its bands make it common to many reporters, but an unusual one (10 or more reports, none acted on) can still let a moderator who revealed that reporter once recognise them on other cases without a further reveal, and so can the wording of their reports (T44's residual). With one moderator, "a different human" is unavailable and appeals wait; a re-examination may be decided by the person who decided the original (section 2). Ids and times stay in the chain for ever (T56's residual). A notice's own text can identify its writer to staff (T44's residual). The freeze and the seal: anyone can freeze what a case reaches by sending a report of this kind through the report form, within the published limits, and an API under an attacker's control can freeze anything by inserting a suspect item; either is a denial of staff viewing and of deletion and, with the viewing, of every other procedure about those images until an admin answers (6.1, the freeze; section 2, the path's row). Such an API can also read any stored file and request any file's deletion, frozen or sealed, until 3d's quarantine (`apps/api/src/media/files.ts:181`; the worker obeys every request and has no database); it can rewrite `posts.body`, which the snapshot of a post case copied and an account case did not; and it can record a decision with a confirmation it made up, an admin's answer or `unseal_media` included, which `audit-verify` detects afterwards and nothing in the database prevents. It cannot delete a frozen row, change an attached image's alt text, or erase a frozen post's snapshot. Before a seal only the rule that staff never open a reported post or account in the app (6.1, the freeze) keeps a staff member from seeing a reported image there, nothing on the server records a look in the app, and after a seal the app still shows what the case does not reach: other accounts' images that a reported account reposted or quoted, the post a reported post replies to and its replies, which the post's page shows with their images, and images posted since the seal. A notice on another ground, or a rule report, whose words describe such images is not suspect until someone opens a finding citing R2 (runbook), so staff can open its images until then. The status page of a notice on this path shows a deleted post's item as waiting where a link that names no row is answered automatically, which tells whoever holds the token that the id exists (T43); an item that names an account by a handle it has left waits the same way, which tells that the handle still names an account and never which handle that account holds now (7.2). On every other ground an old handle is stored as an item whose id names nothing, and such links are de-duplicated by their named handles, so neither a stored id nor a de-duplication ties it to its account (7.1 step 4). A table added later that holds images (3b's import, 2d's avatars and share cards) must join a gate: the enumeration tests fail on a new method of the media repository, not on a new table. `text-verify` detects a sealed image row that the migrator destroyed or a restore lost (7.13). A removed or sealed image stays at Cloudflare's edge and in browsers for up to five minutes until 3d's purge.

**Privacy.** Notifier contacts live in the auth schema, encrypted, out of the API role's reach, and are deleted on a published schedule; a signed-in notifier's contact is their account. The CSAM path stores no account and no contact; its limits count in Valkey only (whether that is identification data is on counsel's list, G9 5.3). The access log records staff, not users. Reporter summaries are computed from the reporter's own rows, which their export contains (G9 3.9). The logger gains `salt` and `token` (already) among its redacted keys (`apps/api/src/lib/logger.ts:11`), so neither a text's salt nor a notice token reaches a log; the status page's tokens travel in a POST body, and the mail's link puts the mail token in the address's fragment, which no server log sees.

## 12. Testing

- **Unit**: the contracts' digests (`textDigest` against vectors shared with the SQL CHECK test, including texts with line feeds, non-ASCII text, emoji and the longest text; `snapshotDigest` round-trips the snapshot answer's JSON); `appealDeadline` against PostgreSQL's `appeal_deadline` on every month end of two years, a leap year included; `laneFor` over every rule, category and the CSAM path; the reporter summary's bands at each boundary (1 and 12 months; 1, 2, 9 and 10 items; no decided item, none acted on, just under half and exactly half; an item that reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` counted in `answeredUnseen` and left out of the decided ones, so that a reporter whose only answered item reads it is `none_decided`) and its letters given per case; `NoticeRequest`'s strictness (refused: a CSAM notice with a name, a signed-in notice with an email, eleven targets, a CSAM notice with two `account` targets, an off-pattern link, a rule report or an illegal-ground notice citing R2; accepted: a rule notice with a `turnstileToken`, a CSAM notice without explanation or bona fide, a CSAM notice with keyword `abuse`); the step-up params schemas (strict, no transform, refinements kept through `.extend({ stepUp })`, unsorted or repeated actions refused, two post measures refused, a correction that carries a ground or facts refused, a final `kept` or `reversed`, an interim `no_violation`, a correction `restricted` and a review `restricted` refused, a review holding a measure refused, `categoryKeyword` with a rule ground refused, the C7 walk); `FindingRequest` refusing every ground but a rule; link parsing (`links.ts`: hosts, paths, case, trailing slashes, a handle that is not a handle); the overlap check (`overlap.ts`: whitespace, case, a 31-character run allowed, 32 refused, notes and `seal_declined` texts exempt, re-examination statements covered); `statementParagraphs` for every outcome, ground, measure and kind, both rulebook versions and the redress text each kind and outcome gets, each with the six Art. 17(3) elements present (practice-3c 11.6), among them an interim decision with the interim text, a correction of an interim decision, a final no-action decision that ends measures taken while deciding, the `restore` and `restore_media` sentences, the field-restore sentence and the field sentence after an interim reset ended by a closing decision, each with and without its 30-day sentence, and none for a field the closing decision resets itself, each `noEffect` sentence, and the seal's sentences for a post case and an account case, with and without deleted posts, while deciding and for good, with the waiting sentence of a seal for good; `reversedOn` null for an interim decision whose measures ended with its case; the WebP header check (a RIFF that is not WEBP, each chunk kind, an oversize edge, a size that differs from the row's); the console's pure parts (router with case paths, `parseCaseId`, `staffImageSrc`, the draft slot, the params builder, the hidden-characters view); the architecture test's new module and rules, each with a test that catches a deliberate violation; source scans: the console's two link sites and one image site, and the author-side files never naming the intake tables (section 11); `postAccess` over every stored state; `smtpMailer`'s errors: `EENVELOPE` on `RCPT TO` with `550 5.1.1 <a@b>: Recipient address rejected: User unknown`, with `550-5.1.1` on the first line of a multi-line reply, and with `556 5.1.10` is `MailRefused`; `RCPT TO` with `554 5.7.1 <a@b>: Relay access denied`, with `553 5.1.8 <f@g>: Sender address rejected`, with a bare `550 rejected`, and with 450, `MAIL FROM` with 550, `EAUTH` with 535, `EMESSAGE` on `DATA` with 554 and `ECONNECTION` are plain `MailUnavailable`; `smtpTransportOptions` sets `connectionTimeout` 10000, `greetingTimeout` 10000, `socketTimeout` 30000 and `dnsTimeout` 10000 for `smtp://` and `smtps://` URLs (the two existing cases in `apps/api/test/mail/smtp.test.ts` gain the four members).
- **The tables (P9)**: `OUTCOME_PHRASES` has exactly one phrase per `item_outcome` value, `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE` follows the outcome of exactly the items about an account that are not suspect and whose answering decision holds no `seal_media`, `QUOTE_ITEM_SCOPE` of exactly the items that are not suspect about a post whose `quote_of_id` is set, and `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE` of exactly the items about an account that are suspect or that a decision holding `seal_media` answered, a rule report merged into a sealed account case among them; a suspect item is worded from `SUSPECT_OUTCOME_PHRASES` and never from `OUTCOME_PHRASES`' four words for a finding alone (`no_violation`, `not_evident`, `restricted_rule` and `restricted_legal` without `SUSPECT_NO_SEAL`); an item about a post that is not suspect and that a decision whose `unseen` is true answered `no_violation` or `not_evident` is worded with `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` and never with those two phrases, on the status page, in the app's list and in a mail's line alike, and no other item is; `SEAL_DRAFTS` holds its eight texts, and the console's composer and the preview are held to them; the app's and the console's message tables have one entry per `ModerationErrorCode` they can meet; the terms' moderation sections, rendered, name every `MEASURES` label, every outcome phrase's meaning and every number of `NOTICE_LIMITS`, `CSAM_NOTICE_MAX_ACCOUNTS`, `SUSPECT_EARLIER_MONTHS`, `DAILY_LIMITS` and `RETENTION`, and no measure or period the constants lack (a strike, a retired handle, an order's preservation); and, in Task 12, a docs test holds the README moderation section's numbers (limits, response times, periods) to the same constants.
- **Integration (database)**, as `korbat_api` unless said, and run on a fresh database and on one migrated to 0013 and committed before the later migrations run as one batch (section 6; `scripts/test-db.mjs --fresh` and `--upgrade-from 13`, Task 1):
  - every grant and refusal of 6.5, and the function pins of 6.5; each CHECK written from a contracts constant held to its constant with `pg_get_constraintdef`; the forced columns ignoring supplied values;
  - the attach trigger (G3 L3 to L12 as tests: two first reports merging, a report against a closing case retried into a new one, opposite-order notices sorted, the naive path retried, the reporter-once index (a second rule report by one reporter on one case refused; that reporter's later illegal-ground notice on the same case accepted), no-case items only `not_available`); re-examination items accepted for a released no-action item within its window, with the original's target, position and lane whatever was supplied, and refused (`reexamination_not_open`) for a restricted item, an unreleased one, a re-examination, one past its window with the clock moved, and an erased intake; a second refused (`reexamination_exists`); a re-examination racing `erase_intake` in both orders: refused, or the erasure refused (`not_due`); `suspect` forced from the intake whatever was supplied; `named_handle` forced null for a post target whatever was supplied, kept for an account target and copied to a re-examination, and an account item without one refused by `intake_items_named_handle` (Task 1); `intake_items_quote`: a suspect item on a quoting post alone refused at commit (`quote_item_missing`), with the quoted post's item committed, an added item without its origin refused, a re-examination and a link that names no row passing, and a re-examination of an origin refused without one of its added item when that is open to re-examination;
  - the checks of `intakes` (a CSAM intake with `alleges_illegal` true and neither explanation nor bona fide accepted, an illegal-ground notice without them refused; a CSAM intake with a null keyword accepted, and one with a reporter or a contact refused; a user intake citing R2 refused, signed in or out, a finding citing R2 accepted; a finding alleging illegality refused);
  - a snapshot written by the intake transaction and one written by the snapshot route recompute from their stored rows to their digests; a capture of a post whose `erased_at` is set holds an empty body and null alt texts and is shown, not withheld, by `snapshot_withheld`;
  - `post_media_keep` (gate K, at database level; the two-session cases as in section 3's lab): a direct delete skips a row a hold retains and a row of a frozen post, and `RETURNING` omits it; the ordered delete and a plain `delete ... where post_id = $1`, each started while an intake holds the rows `FOR KEY SHARE` with its suspect item uncommitted, wait and then delete nothing, for a post target and for an account target; a delete that commits first leaves the intake locking nothing, without an error; a cascade from a deleted post row and from a deleted profile row, as the migrator, raises `23001` for a row frozen through its post's item, through its author's account's item and through a seal, and for an erased image of a post under its own item; a delete of an attached row inside a REPEATABLE READ or SERIALIZABLE transaction raises `read_committed_only`; `delete from posts` and `delete from profiles` are refused for `korbat_api`; `post_media_guard`: a change of a set `post_id`, an attach to another account's post and a change of `owner_id` refused for `korbat_api` and for the migrator, a change of `alt_text`, `position`, `width`, `height` or `blurhash` on an attached row refused for `korbat_api`, and `attach` for the post's author passing; the freeze's predicate as far as no decision is needed (no item; an unanswered suspect item on the post, on its author's account, and on the post through an added item; an item that is not suspect: false; an item of a notice on the law alone, with no rule: `suspect` false, never null, and the predicate false); `media_holds_apply`, including a release of one case's hold racing a new hold of another case on the same image (the image stays held); `hold` racing `dropDisposable` on a deleted post's images whose hold just ended, in both orders (the hold skips the deleted rows, or the rows stay held and are not deleted; never a foreign-key error or a deadlock); `dropDisposable` over 60 disposable rows: three batches (25, 25, 10), each committed with its audit row and its files requested before the next batch, and over 60 rows of which 40 are of a frozen post: the other 20 deleted in one batch and the step ending; the files of a batch whose request was skipped are deleted by one reconcile pass without `MEDIA_RECONCILE=force`; two intakes naming the same two posts in opposite orders, concurrently (no deadlock); the author's deletion (`detachAll`) and the account's purge (`purgeOwner`) of a post with two images whose position order differs from their id order, racing an intake that locks them through an account, in both orders (no deadlock: every attached row kept, or none locked); `redact_r4_body` of a `remove_media` of the image with the higher id racing such an intake, in both orders (no deadlock);
  - **the derivation** (P1): `moderation_apply` against a reference implementation in the test over generated sequences of measures, reversals, case closures and erasures on one post across three cases, in every order of arrival (the level and every image's state equal the strongest measure in force); and, as named cases: case 2's interim withhold stays when case 1's removal of the post is reversed (the post ends `withheld`); two cases' removals, either reversed, leave it `removed`, and both reversed concurrently in both orders leave it `visible`; a lifted seal while another case's removal of the post is in force leaves the post `removed` and its images not sealed; an interim withhold ends when its case closes, with no restore, and stays while a final decision leaves the case open; a deleted post's `state` stays `deleted_by_author` while its `moderation_level` follows the ledger; `erased_at` keeps a post `removed` after every reversal; `withhold_media` and `remove_media` likewise; and the images' states over `seal_posts`, against a reference implementation that reads `seal_posts` and applies the view's clause for a seal taken while deciding: every image of a listed post `sealed` while a seal in force lists it, whatever the post's level; a seal taken while deciding ended by the answer to its suspect items while an item of another kind keeps the case open; a second seal with a list of its own; a seal for good after one taken while deciding, the images never served in between;
  - **the decision triggers**: version, range, snapshot, texts, parties and links, the caps, the ground, the source, consistency, the audit link (each member, another case, another decider, an extra or a missing action, actions inserted in another order), an action of another transaction's decision; reversals: the target found from the reviewed decision or the case's interim measure, never a choice among several; a reversal of a measure not in force, a second lift, a second interim withhold or `withhold_media` of one kind and image in force in a case, and two post measures in one decision, each refused (`invalid_decision`); a second interim `reset_bio`, in a case already holding one in force, after the author set the bio again: accepted on a fresh snapshot, and both listed in the closing decision's `endedMeasures`; a `restore_bio` in an interim or final decision refused, and in a correction of the resetting decision accepted; P2's measures on any level: a final `remove` of a post another case removed, of a post its author deleted, a second seal of images already sealed and an interim withhold of a removed post accepted, with `no_effect` `already` or `deleted`, and `author_mail` `none` only for the interim measure on the deleted post; the freeze's predicate as a table, with decisions: a post answered without a seal (false); under a seal taken while deciding (true; false once the suspect items are answered, also while an item of another kind keeps the case open; when a new suspect item then arrives the post is frozen again through that item while the old seal stays ended and its images stay served until a person seals again); a post frozen through its own added item still frozen after the quoting post's item is answered; under a seal for good after the case closed (true); after `unseal_media` (false); a post the account made after an account's seal, once the item is answered (false); after a re-examination (true again); the seal's rules: a `seal_media` refused on a decision whose `suspect` is false, with another rule than R2 and another category than Protection of minors, and on a case that reaches no image (`invalid_decision`), accepted while deciding from a moderator on a post case and on an account case, and a second one in the same case accepted with a list of its own; `seal_posts` holding one row for each post with an image row, with their numbers, a deleted post's kept images included, an upload on no post and another account's post not; `no_effect` `deleted` on a deleted target, `already` for a second seal while deciding over the same posts, and null for the seal for good that follows its case's seal taken while deciding; a `remove` of the post's text beside a `seal_media` in one decision accepted; the answer: `suspect` forced for an interim decision on a case holding an unanswered suspect item and for a final one that answers one; `unseen` forced whatever was supplied (Task 2): true for a final decision on a post case while `post_frozen(post)` is true, through a suspect item of its own case, through its author's account's case (a moderator's no-action decision on a rule report there, accepted without an admin) and through a seal for good in force, and false for an interim decision, a correction, a decision on an account case and a final decision on a post that is not frozen; a final decision through an earlier suspect item while a later one of the case is unanswered refused (`case_changed`), for an admin too, and one through both accepted and ending the seal taken while deciding; the range of an admin's answer (Task 2 for the database half): on a case with a rule item, then a suspect item, then a second rule item, an admin's final decision whose `through_seq` is the suspect item's `seq` is accepted, answers the first two items and leaves the third unanswered and the case open; an item that committed while the seal's decision waited for its locks freezing and alerting on either side of the comparison; `ended_interim` and `author_mail` `pending` set by `decisions_apply` for a final decision without a seal that ends a seal its author was told of, also while an item of another kind keeps the case open; an R2 citation refused on a decision whose `suspect` is false; an item of a CSAM notice merged into an R5 case about a post without images: a removal under R5 accepted from an admin, the suspect item answered `restricted_rule`, and a `seal_media` refused there; `needs_admin` forced for a final decision that answers a suspect item or an R5 item or cites R2 or R5, and for a correction of such a decision, each refused to a moderator (`admin_required`) and accepted for an admin; a moderator's interim `withhold` of a frozen post, and a moderator's early lift of it, accepted; `unseal_media` refused in an interim or a final decision, refused to a moderator in a correction, also of a seal that moderator took, and accepted for an admin in a correction (Task 2) and in a review (Task 8, since a review needs 0017), with `needs_admin` set; a demotion from admin to moderator committing during an R2 decision (the decision is refused, or the demotion waits for its commit); the sweep deleting a text between step 4 and the confirmation (`text_not_found`); `staff_target`: an interim withhold of a staff account's post and an interim reset of its bio commit, a final `remove` and a `seal_media` for good on it are refused, a `seal_media` while deciding commits, and after `grant-role` to `user` the final `remove` commits; telling: `ended_interim` and `author_mail` `pending` for a closing final decision while an interim measure is in force, for a final decision holding a reversal of one, and for a moderator's correction of an R5 interim decision (accepted without an admin); `decisions_reversal_plain`, `decisions_keyword` and `decisions_law_no_rule`;
  - **the posts' guard**: as `korbat_api`, each refusal of section 11; the migrator's `moderation_apply` and `redact_r4_body` passing; leaving `deleted_by_author` refused as the migrator; a counter update not firing it;
  - `append_confirmed_audit` (G2's S1 to S4, E5, E6 and A3 as concurrency tests: revocation and take-down in both orders, a held head past the expiry, an expired challenge answering ROLLBACK at COMMIT, account before key);
  - `release_outcomes`: an item answered before the cut-off released with its outcome as of the cut-off; a reversal before the cut-off releasing `partly_reversed` or `reversed` with `outcome_mail` `pending` again; one after the cut-off waiting for the next; `outcome_mail` left as it is when the shown outcome does not change; a cut-off in the future refused; `korbat_api` refused writing `outcome_sent_at`, `shown_outcome` or `revised_sent_at`;
  - **appeals and reviews**: windows at the clamp; an appeal of an interim decision, a no-action decision, a review or a correction refused (`appeal_not_open`); an appeal of a final restriction a correction reversed in full refused, and of one reversed in part accepted; an appeal racing a full correction, in both orders, refused or answered `reversed`; the unique index under two concurrent appeals; an intake into an open case not waiting for an appeal's lock; an appeal racing `erase_case_evidence` and `redact_r4_body` at its deadline, in both orders (the appeal refused, or the erasure refused `not_due`); a review answering exactly its one appeal (`appeal_id` forced; `case_changed` when none is pending; a params `appealId` that differs refused at COMMIT); `reviewer_conflict` for a decider and for the finder; `review_needs_admin` true for a decision citing R2 or holding a `seal_media`, and false for an R5 removal, for a decision that answered a suspect item without a seal, and for an R1 removal of a post that a later suspect item freezes; a moderator's review of an R5 restriction accepted; `appeal_reviewer_state` with one staff account, with two who both decided, with a second staff account linked to a reporter of the case (`none_eligible`), with an eligible one (`available`), and null for an answered appeal; a correction reversing everything answering the pending appeal `reversed`, a partial one leaving it; the appeals CHECK and the re-examination statement CHECK (no statement, a statement with its digest, an erased one; a wrong digest and a digest without a statement on an unerased row refused);
  - a reveal confirming its reason text, and one whose `reasonDigest` names no saved text of its case refused (`text_not_found`); `sweep_staff_texts` refusing an argument less than 24 hours old and never deleting a confirmed text, a note, a `seal_declined` text or one under 24 hours old; `delete_access_log` erasing, never deleting, a reveal's reason; `text-verify` passing over a reveal older than 12 months; `text-verify` recomputing image states from the ledger, `seal_posts` and the items, and, for every seal in force, checking that each `seal_posts` row's post still has `images` image rows and reporting a difference like a state that differs;
  - **retention functions**: `case_evidence_due_at` null while open and while a restriction is pending appeal, a closed no-action case due a day after its last snapshot or close, a field reset's case not due before 30 days after the lift, and a frozen post's snapshots never erased by `erase_case_evidence` or `redact_r4_body`, in whatever case they are and under each kind of reach, while the case's notes go and its holds are released (gate E); `intake_due_at` null under each of its conditions and 30 days after the last release otherwise; every erase function refusing what is not due (`not_due`); a correction racing `erase_case_evidence` on a due case: naming the old snapshot, committed before the erasure or refused `snapshot_erased`, and naming a snapshot taken in the last day, committed with the erasure refused; `redact_r4_body` raising `invalid_decision` for an R3 removal and for an interim R4 withhold, `not_due` while the author's appeal is pending or the window is open, acting on a removal of a post deleted before or after it, setting `posts.erased_at` only for a post removal, answering false while the post is frozen, leaving `redacted_at` null when a suspect item about the author's account commits between its check and its erasures and finishing after an answer without a seal, and a later reversal changing nothing visible; `erase_snapshots` refused to `korbat_api`; it, `redact_r4_body` and a delete of an attached image row raising `read_committed_only` under REPEATABLE READ and SERIALIZABLE; `users_deleted_mark` as `korbat_auth` (a back-dated mark becomes `now()`; clearing or changing a set mark refused); `erase_statement_texts` refused and `forget_reporter` changing nothing for an account whose profile `korbat_api` marked deleted while its auth row is live; `has_schema_privilege` false.
- **Integration (flows)**:
  - **intake**: signed in, signed out with and without contact, CSAM signed in and out, a signed-in illegal-ground notice followed by a CSAM report by the same account about the same post (both answered 201; the CSAM item stands with no reporter; the case moves to lane 0 with `imagesAllowed` false), findings and the findings limit (the 51st finding of a UTC day refused with `rate_limited` and nothing written, another staff account's accepted, the overview's `findingsToday` counting both), multi-item with duplicates and dead links (Task 4: two unknown handles in one notice are two items, each with a `target_id` that names nothing; one unknown handle named twice is one item; an old handle on a rule ground is such an item too, its `target_id` not its account's), a case closed between the intake's insert and its read, limits per account and per network with IPv6 /64 grouping, the receipt's states, the contact stored after the commit and its failure (the receipt recorded `no_address`, `contactLost` true, a reveal of it refused); a signed-out notice and a signed-out CSAM notice answered 201 with `ref` and `token` while the mailer never resolves, the receipt recorded once it answers and `settle()` resolves; `/api/notices/contact` refusing the notice token and accepting the mail token; a retried receipt carrying the same page link; confirm lifting a suppression; a fresh notice naming a confirmed or a suppressed address answered exactly as one naming an address korbat never saw; the races through the routes (6.1, gate K): a CSAM notice against the author's deletion and against the account's purge, each in both orders, for a post target and for an account target (no error, no deadlock; every image row the notice reaches kept when the notice locks first, none when the delete does);
  - **liveness and the path's flow** (P3; 6.1, the freeze): an R1 or illegal-ground report about a post its author deleted answered `not_available` with no case and no hold, and the same about a purged account; a signed-out CSAM notice about a live image post: a lane-0 case, a snapshot, holds, `imagesAllowed` false on it and on a closed R1 case about the same post, and one `moderation_urgent`; about a post its author deleted before the notice, with an image a closed case's hold still keeps, with none left, and blanked by the account's purge: each a lane-0 case on the tombstone that waits for an admin, never `not_available`; the same with a console finding citing R2; about a link that names no row: `not_available`, answered automatically; about a post that quotes another: two items and two cases in lane 0, one alert that counts both, the status page listing the second as "The post that item 1 quotes ..., added by korbat", each with its own outcome, also when the quoting post was deleted before the notice and when it has no image of its own (its case then reaches no image and is refused a seal, while the quoted post's case is sealed); a notice naming both posts adding nothing; two named posts quoting the same post adding one item at the lower position plus 10; about an account: one case that reaches every image on the account's posts, a deleted post's kept images included and an upload on no post not; the author then deletes one of those posts and, in a second run, the account is purged: the rows stay, are served to nobody and are in no view and no export; a purged account named by its handle: a case, not `not_available`; an account named by a handle it has left (Task 4 for the notice, Task 5 for the finding, whose route it builds): a CSAM notice, and a finding citing R2, each open a lane-0 case on that account, freeze its posts' images and send the alert, and are never answered `not_available`, while an R1 report that names the same old handle is answered `not_available`; the address shown back, for every ground: for an account that changed its handle after a report, the status page and the receipt (Task 4), the `notice_update` line (Task 6) and the reporter's list in the app (Task 7) show the handle as named, never the later one; a CSAM notice naming two accounts refused (`invalid_request`), also when the two handles are an account's old and present one; from there a moderator seals while deciding (the author mailed with the interim redress, every image `sealed`, served to nobody and a placeholder in every view), a moderator's final decision and a moderator's `unseal_media` are refused, and an admin seals for good (the author mailed with the appeal and the waiting sentence, the items released in the `sealed` phrase of `SUSPECT_OUTCOME_PHRASES`); in a second run the admin answers without a seal (the seal taken while deciding ends, its author is told, the images are served again, `moderation_csam_lifted` goes to every staff account with `dismissed`, the items are released in the `no_action` phrase, and at the next retention round the kept rows of the deleted posts are deleted and their files requested); an account's item answered without a seal after a finding sealed one of its posts: the status page shows the `no_action` phrase with `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE`; a suspect item answered by a removal of the post's text under R1: the R1 phrase with `SUSPECT_NO_SEAL`; each phrase read on the status page for a post, an account, an added item and a deleted post;
  - **deciding**: each measure's effect on posts, images and fields through `moderation_apply`; interim then final; an interim withhold ended by a closing no-action final decision (mailed to the author, listed, the statement naming the ended measure); a decision that answers through a seq while a new item arrives; the range of an admin's answer through the composer (Task 6; 7.5 step 1): on a case with a rule item, then a suspect item, then a second rule item, the console sends the suspect item's `seq` as `throughSeq`, the admin's answer covers the first two items and leaves the third open, and the page says so; two moderators deciding one case; the author's deletion during a decision; **the contention lab** (section 2, Lock order), in Task 2 a database lab that issues its statements itself, as SQL, against migration 0016's schema and with no code of a later task (the ordered delete by `owner_id` as 7.10 gives it, the intake's lock statement as 7.1 gives it, a seal's lock and writes as 6.2 gives them), and extended with `redact_r4_body` in Task 9: likes, follows, deletions and an account purge against decisions, with no deadlock but the purge's, which the retry absorbs; and `redact_r4_body`, an intake's `capturePost` and locks, a seal, and `decisions_apply`'s calls when a case closes or a seal taken while deciding ends, each against `moderation_apply` on the same post (P1 security condition (h), build condition (8)); on a quiet machine, an account's seal at 1,000 and at 5,000 image posts, held to a small part of the challenge's ten minutes, a seal for good that follows one taken while deciding on such an account (each image row written twice), the purge of an account with 5,000 and with 20,000 image rows, and a seal against a notice that names a post and its author's account (no deadlock: a seal locks no post); Task 3's lab of the intake's lock statement (`MediaForCases.hold` with an owner pair, 7.1 step 4) at 5,000 and at 20,000 attached rows, run at the repository; a reset on a purged profile writing nothing; a lifted reset, and an interim reset ended by a closing no-action decision, each writing nothing, their statements carrying the field sentence and the link, and the old value shown on the resetting decision's screen for 30 days, also when the lifting decision named a fresh snapshot holding the placeholder; the author setting a reset bio again through `editProfile`, accepted;
  - **every reader, by enumeration** (gates V and S): `serve` (the author's decision view among its callers: it is no list behind `forPosts` and draws its images through the app's image route, 7.6), each list behind `forPosts` (the single post, a thread, each timeline, Explore, the profile lists, likes, bookmarks, a quote embed, the export's list), the export's files, `status`, and `staffImage` on the suspicion's case, on another open case and on a closed case about the post. For a frozen post whose images are not sealed, staff are refused and every other reader is unchanged. For sealed images nobody is served, the author included; every list carries the placeholder, with `sealed` only for the author and without a `blurhash`; `status` answers without the `blurhash`; and a sealed image of a post that is itself withheld or removed answers 404 from `serve` to its author too, the seal being tested before the author's branch. For a deleted post whose rows a freeze keeps (through its own item, its author's account's item and an added item), the author's export names no image under that post and holds no file of it, no view lists one, and `find` and `status` answer its owner as for a row that does not exist, as they do for an image whose own or whose post's `erased_at` is set. A seal that commits while an export streams leaves no sealed file in the archive from that point on. The test holds the list of readers beside the list of deleters, as one record over the media repository's methods (`keyof MediaRepo`), and fails when `createMediaRepo` has a method on neither;
  - **reveals**: each refusal of 7.3's Revealing a source; the 11th reveal of a UTC day refused with nothing written (no access row, the reason text unconfirmed) while another staff account's is accepted; `moderation_reveal` to every staff account with the count and the limit, and a failed send changing nothing; the access row and the audit row committed together; no identity answered when the commit fails;
  - **every deleter, by enumeration** (gate K), and the case routes: for a frozen post under each kind of reach (its own item, its author's account's item, an added item, a seal for good), every method of the media repository that deletes is run (`deleteUnattached`, `detachAll`, `purgeOwner`, `deleteUnattachedBefore`, `deleteRow`, `dropDisposable`): each leaves the rows, returns no id of them and writes no request file (`files.deletionRequests()`); then through the flows: the author's deletion, the purge by the request and by the job, the sweeper a day on, the reconcile pass, a released hold, every step of the retention job with every due time passed, and an R4 redaction made final; a closed R1 case on a post that then receives a CSAM notice serves none of its images while the suspect item is unanswered, and serves them again once an admin answers it without a seal; the same for a notice about a post quoting it and about the author's account; `staff_recused` on every case route when the subject is linked or related, never because only a reporter is; a reporter who follows the sole staff member's linked account not stopping that member's interim withhold, and a subject who follows, blocks or replied to it not stopping them opening or deciding the case; the snapshot route answering no content; a person at the daily limit who takes a snapshot of a case not opened that day getting its id, the case read refused with no access row; the case answer's `suspicion`: `open`, the counts of `reach` before and after a seal and after the account posts an image, `earlier` counting an answer without a seal about the target and about its author within 12 months and not one with a seal, `imagesOpenedOn` null until an image of the post is served to staff, then the day of the newest `image_viewed` row on any case about the post, and null on an account case, `quotedCaseId` on a quoting post's item and `quotedByPosition` on an added item, and a seal's `sealed` list capped at `SEAL_LIST_MAX`; a reporter one of whose released items reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` summarised with it in `answeredUnseen` and not among the decided items of `actedOn`; an admin's correction that lifts a seal and an admin's final decision without a seal on a case holding a suspect item each send `moderation_csam_lifted` to every staff account and appear in the overview's `protectionLifted` as `unsealed` and `dismissed`; a seal taken while deciding appears in `interimSeals` until it ends, and a `seal_declined` text in `sealsDeclined`; a seal and a kept review send no `moderation_csam_lifted`; a failed send changes nothing; the source pins (`post_frozen`, a delete of `post_media`, `requestDeletion`, `openImage`), each with a test that catches a deliberate violation;
  - **re-examination**: a notifier's re-examination of a no-action item through either token, joining the open case or opening one, decided by a final decision and released at the next dispatch; a restriction then reaching the author, who may appeal it once; a signed-in reporter's re-examination from Your reports; a re-examination on the CSAM path with no account or contact, in lane 0, sending `moderation_urgent` and needing an admin; refused past the window, twice, and for a restricted item; one about a target with nothing live answered `not_available`; the console marking it for the original decider, whose decision is not refused; an added item re-examined by its own position; a re-examination of an origin that re-examines its added item too, and one whose added item is unanswered re-examining the origin alone; an answer that does not spend the re-examination (Task 8): a rule report about a post that an admin's answer to a suspect item of its case answered without action reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` with `unseen` true, its re-examination is accepted, and when that re-examination is answered the same way a re-examination of it is accepted too, each counted against the daily limit, the same for a rule report that a moderator answered without action while the post was frozen through its author's account's case, while a re-examination that a decision whose `unseen` is false answered is refused one (`reexamination_not_open`), and such a report about an account keeps the ordinary phrase and its one re-examination;
  - **appeals and reviews**: with one and two moderators; a review and a correction that lift a seal, accepted for an admin and refused to a moderator; a kept review mailed and listed; an author's appeal result shown at once; an appeal of a decision citing R2, and of one that sealed images, with `needsAdmin` true, the latter with the seal's waiting sentence; an appeal of an R1 removal of a post that a later suspect item freezes with `needsAdmin` false; the chip of a removed post naming the removing decision after a `kept` review, after a later case's removal of the same post (the newer removal named) and, once an R4 redaction erased it and every measure was reversed, naming the redacted decision; `GET /api/moderation/decisions/:id` answering 404 for a decision with `author_mail` `none`; a moderator's early lift of an R5 interim withhold accepted and told;
  - **dispatch**: receipts, statements, the daily batch at the hour, folded receipts, suppression, `outcome_sent_at` and the re-examination window, `forgetNotifierAfter`; an item reversed by a review mailed with its new outcome and shown on the status page and in the list only after the release; a line carrying `ALREADY_NOT_SHOWN`; what the sender of another report reads after an admin's answer to a suspect item (Task 6 for the mail and the status answer, Task 7 for the app's list): a notice of illegality about a post, answered without action by that answer, carries `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` in its mail's line and on its status page, with `unseen` true, and a signed-in rule report the same words in Your reports; the same notice about an account keeps the ordinary phrase with `ACCOUNT_ITEM_SCOPE`; and a rule report about an account that an answer with a seal answered carries the `sealed` phrase with `ACCOUNT_SUSPECT_SCOPE`; a rule report on a post frozen through its author's account's case, and one on a post whose images are sealed for good, each answered without action by a moderator, read `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION` on the status page and in Your reports (Task 6 for the phrase, Task 7 for the list), and the same report answered once the post is no longer frozen reads the ordinary phrase; a suppressed address still getting its account's `moderation_decision` and not its report mails; `reportMailStopped` and the resume; a relay failure, and a restart after `outcome_sent_at` was set, leaving lines `pending` for the next round; an unconfirmed address's daily update; a folded receipt riding in the next daily update, alone when there is nothing else, and two folds on one day in one update; two notices naming one address making one update; a case still undecided at 13 months keeping its notifier's contact; a refused receipt, author mail and outcome line each recorded `failed` at once; an unavailable one retried, then `failed` with the clock five days on; a contact whose only lines are `failed` not moved by step 3(b); an undecryptable contact not stopping the round or other addresses' updates; a daily step whose (b) fails releasing nothing new when it runs again and sending no outcome mail until (b) has finished; with (b) failing for six days, no line recorded `failed` before step 4 tried it; a decision made after the cut-off released on the next dispatch day; the dispatch hour moved from 07 to 23 after the day's release, that day's later decisions released at 23:00 and `releaseOverdue` staying 0; a partial correction of a decision that removed two images, lifting one, making every item `partly_reversed`, then a review lifting the other making them `reversed`; a signed-out update carrying the same page link as its receipt, and a re-examination through that link accepted; a notice of illegality with a contact about a quoting post, its line, its status page and its `scope` carrying `QUOTE_ITEM_SCOPE`; an item about a post that quotes nothing carrying neither scope; the urgent repeat in its three cases (7.12): a suspect item whose case reaches a served image repeating through a note, a withhold of the post and a field reset, ending with a seal, starting again when the account posts an image, ending with a `seal_declined` text saved after the item arrived, and starting again when the account posts an image after that text, also when a seal was taken in between (the text's time against `newestServedAt`); a suspect item whose case reaches no served image ending with a note saved after it arrived, and not with one saved before it; an R5 item on a visible post never ended by a note; each `NoticeUpdateLine` field set as section 5 defines it (a re-examinable no-action line, a re-examination's answer, a changed outcome, a case-less line, and a line that reads `UNSEEN_NO_ACTION`, which carries `unseen` and gets no "final within korbat", also when it answers a re-examination that was asked again before a retried mail went out);
  - **retention**: each row of 6.6 with the clock moved: a no-action case's snapshots erased a day after it closes; a restriction's evidence kept until it is final for its author; an intake record erased 30 days after its last release, kept while a restriction it led to is pending appeal, and a reversal on that appeal reaching the signed-in reporter; an R4 removal (also of a post its author deleted before or after, and followed by the purge) redacted at finality: the body blank, `erased_at` set, every snapshot of the post in every case erased, the image rows deleted by `dropDisposable` once no hold retains them, the decision screens saying "Content erased when the R4 decision became final", `access-request` printing no R4 text; an R4 `remove_media` and an R4 `reset_bio` made final the same way; an R4 removal of an image post made final while a suspect item about the post waits unanswered in the same open case, then again with the R4 case closed and the item in a case about its author's account, each run through the retention job with the clock moved past the decision's `decision_final_at`: `redact_r4_body` answers false and no audit row is written (the body, the images and every snapshot of the post kept, `redactions` 0), after an admin's seal for good it goes on answering false, and after an admin's answer without a seal it erases as usual; a suspect item about the author's account committed between the redaction's check and its erasures: the body blank, the snapshots kept, `redacted_at` null, and the redaction finished at the first round after the answer; a closed R1 case holding the images of a post its author then deleted, run through the retention job with the clock moved past its due time while a suspect item waits unanswered in a case about the author's account (again with the account purged, and again with the item on the post itself as an added item): the R1 case's notes are erased and its holds released, its snapshot of the post is kept by `erase_snapshots`, the image rows stay, no file is requested, and `caseEvidence` is 0; after an admin answers without a seal, the next round erases that snapshot, and the rows are deleted and their files requested; an R4 `remove_media` of one image made final while the case is open with an unanswered R1 item about another image of the post: only the removed image's hold is released, and the author's deletion of the post keeps the other image; `erasureOverdue` counting each of its terms while its step is made to fail, and 0 when every step succeeds; with a post's images sealed for good and the seal's appeal window run, a closed no-action case holding a snapshot of the post is neither read as a candidate nor counted in `caseEvidence` two days later, while the sealing case's notes are erased at its due time; that snapshot is erased at the first round after a review lifts the seal; a case whose notes `erase_case_evidence` erased is no longer a candidate; for an R4 removal whose appeal a review answered naming a fresh snapshot, so that `redact_r4_body` erased the case's snapshots and released its holds before the case was due, the appeal statement is still erased at the case's due time; each step's audit row committing with its erasure, and a step that raises leaving neither; a second round changing nothing and writing no audit row; an R4-redacted image held by a second case not served to its author or to staff, not listed or exported, and not re-held by a later rule report; a correction lifting a final `reset_bio` after the case's copies were erased, on a fresh snapshot: its statement carries the field sentence without the 30-day sentence, and the resetting decision's screen shows the `erased` reason; with `MODERATION_RETENTION` off, no retention round runs and the dispatch job's rounds do;
  - account deletion with open cases, retained images and reports filed (the reporter links forgotten); an account case with an interim `reset_bio`, then the account purged, then the case closed: the bio stays `''` and the display name "Deleted account"; the export's member (an item decided but not released shows no outcome; a post whose images are sealed exported with its text and without those images).
  A fixture `decideForTest(post, state)` makes a real confirmed decision with the software authenticator (`apps/api/test/integration/staff-step-up.ts`) and replaces the 18 direct state writes G4 listed (`http-explore.test.ts:154`, `http-media.test.ts:192,199`, `media-harness.ts:107` (for `withheld` and `removed` only), `social-explore-ranking.test.ts:150-151`, `social-explore-reads.test.ts:45`, `social-flows-posts.test.ts:51,100,125,135`, `social-repo.test.ts:156-157`). Every test that writes a row `audit-verify` must refuse runs in a transaction that rolls back, as 3a's do.
- **HTTP**: every new route's session, staff session, step-up and refusal codes; a signed-out rule report and a signed-out CSAM notice without a token refused with `human_check_failed`, and accepted with Turnstile's test token; `POST /api/notices` from a signed-in session that has not onboarded answering `onboarding_required`; a decision, a review and a reveal whose key is revoked during the transaction answering 401 `staff_session_required` with both staff cookies cleared; the signed-out notice through the app's site with an Origin check and no CSRF token; the console's case routes through the staff gate; the image route's headers; no `Access-Control-*` header on any staff answer (3a's test extended); the route table's lists, its parameters (now `:case`, `:media`, `:size`, `:item`, `:decision` besides `:handle`), its `PERSONAL` check over the new answers, and the step-up coverage test with its named exceptions; a decision that meets a deadlock or a lock timeout retried once with the same confirmation and committed, and a second one, or one met with a minute or less of the challenge left, answering 400 `challenge_expired` with nothing written; a text save whose `digest` does not match refused with `text_invalid` and nothing stored; `POST /api/staff/findings` with a link to a missing post, to a post its author deleted, or to a deleted account answering 404 `not_found` with nothing written and the day's count unchanged, and a finding citing R2 on a deleted post, on a purged account or on an account named by a handle it has left answering 201 whatever is left of it, while a finding under another rule that names such a handle answers `not_found`; a CSAM notice whose links name two accounts answering `invalid_request` with nothing written; the re-examination routes' codes and limits.
